HEADER = r'''import json
import re

from lfx.custom.custom_component.component import Component
from lfx.io import (
    BoolInput,
    DataInput,
    DropdownInput,
    FloatInput,
    HandleInput,
    IntInput,
    MessageInput,
    MultilineInput,
    Output,
    StrInput,
)
from lfx.schema.data import Data
from lfx.schema.message import Message


def _state_of(payload):
    """Unwrap a Langflow Data payload (or list of them) into a GraphState dict.

    Inactive conditional branches emit an empty Data, so the first payload that
    actually carries state wins.
    """
    if payload is None:
        return {}
    items = payload if isinstance(payload, list) else [payload]
    for item in items:
        data = dict(getattr(item, "data", {}) or {})
        if data:
            return data
    return {}


def _switch(state, name, default=True):
    return bool((state.get("switches") or {}).get(name, default))


def _llm_text(llm, prompt):
    """Call the wired Language Model with a single prompt and return its text."""
    response = llm.invoke(prompt)
    content = getattr(response, "content", response)
    if isinstance(content, list):
        content = "".join(str(getattr(part, "text", part)) for part in content)
    return str(content or "").strip()


def _parse_json(text, default):
    """Cheap JSON recovery: strip code fences, take the first balanced object/array."""
    cleaned = re.sub(r"```[a-zA-Z]*", "", text or "").strip()
    start = next((i for i, ch in enumerate(cleaned) if ch in "[{"), -1)
    if start == -1:
        return default
    opener = cleaned[start]
    closer = "]" if opener == "[" else "}"
    depth = 0
    for index in range(start, len(cleaned)):
        if cleaned[index] == opener:
            depth += 1
        elif cleaned[index] == closer:
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0:
                try:
                    return json.loads(cleaned[start:index + 1])
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    return default
    return default


def _chunks_block(chunks):
    return "\n\n".join(
        "[{0}] [Source: {1}]\n{2}".format(index, chunk.get("source", "?"), chunk.get("content", ""))
        for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks)
    )


def _sentences(text):
    return [part.strip() for part in re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", text or "") if part.strip()]


def _field(track):
    """documents -> 'document', learned_qa -> 'learned_qa' (matches GraphState keys)."""
    return "document" if track == "documents" else "learned_qa"
'''


USER_INPUT = HEADER + r'''

class RagUserInput(Component):
    display_name = "user_input"
    description = (
        "Graph entry point. Parses the chat message, detects the reserved commands "
        "(stats / learn / bad / exit), resolves the per-request ENABLE_* switches and "
        "emits the initial GraphState. Mirrors user_input_node + route_user_input."
    )
    icon = "log-in"
    name = "RagUserInput"

    ROUTES = ("to_query", "to_stats", "to_learn", "to_bad", "to_exit")
    COMMANDS = {"stats": "stats", "learn": "learn", "bad": "bad", "exit": "exit", "quit": "exit"}

    inputs = [
        MessageInput(
            name="chat_message",
            display_name="Chat Message",
            info="The Playground message. A bare 'stats' / 'learn' / 'bad' / 'exit' is treated as a command.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_sub_query_generation",
            display_name="ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION",
            value=True,
            info="Drives generate_query_variants and the retry budget in route_after_quality_check.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_compression_validation",
            display_name="ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION",
            value=True,
            info="Drives the NAC/DC validator routers on both compression tracks.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_answer_draft_creation",
            display_name="ENABLE_ANSWER_DRAFT_CREATION",
            value=True,
            info="False routes combine_tracks straight to generate_answer.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_answer_quality_check",
            display_name="ENABLE_ANSWER_QUALITY_CHECK",
            value=True,
            info="False routes generate_draft straight to generate_answer.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_auto_distillation",
            display_name="ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION",
            value=False,
            info="True routes generate_answer through auto_distillation.",
        ),
        IntInput(
            name="retry_limit",
            display_name="LLM_RESPONSE_RETRY_LIMIT",
            value=3,
            advanced=True,
            info="Maximum retrieval retries before the graph gives up and answers without context.",
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="Query State", name="to_query", method="route_query"),
        Output(display_name="cmd stats", name="to_stats", method="route_stats"),
        Output(display_name="cmd learn", name="to_learn", method="route_learn"),
        Output(display_name="cmd bad", name="to_bad", method="route_bad"),
        Output(display_name="cmd exit", name="to_exit", method="route_exit"),
    ]

    def _initial_state(self):
        text = getattr(self.chat_message, "text", self.chat_message) or ""
        query = str(text).strip()
        command = self.COMMANDS.get(query.lower(), "")
        return {
            "user_input": query,
            "query": query,
            "command": command,
            "user_feedback": "",
            "request_id": str(getattr(self, "_id", "")),
            "switches": {
                "ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION": self.enable_sub_query_generation,
                "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION": self.enable_compression_validation,
                "ENABLE_ANSWER_DRAFT_CREATION": self.enable_answer_draft_creation,
                "ENABLE_ANSWER_QUALITY_CHECK": self.enable_answer_quality_check,
                "ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION": self.enable_auto_distillation,
            },
            "retry_limit": self.retry_limit,
            "retry_count": 0,
            "query_variants": [],
            "blocked_variants": [],
            "prior_thumbdowns": [],
            "retrieved_document_chunks": [],
            "retrieved_learned_qa_chunks": [],
            "variants_with_chunks": [],
            "newly_failed_variants": [],
            "compressed_docs": [],
            "answer": "",
        }

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._initial_state()
        command = state.get("command", "")
        route = {
            "stats": "to_stats",
            "learn": "to_learn",
            "bad": "to_bad",
            "exit": "to_exit",
        }.get(command, "to_query")
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "route={0} query={1!r}".format(route, state["query"])
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_query(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_query")

    def route_stats(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_stats")

    def route_learn(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_learn")

    def route_bad(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_bad")

    def route_exit(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_exit")
'''


COMMAND = HEADER + r'''

class RagCommand(Component):
    display_name = "cmd"
    description = (
        "Terminal command branch (cmd_stats / cmd_learn / cmd_bad / cmd_exit). In the "
        "LangGraph app these call the MongoDB feedback store and the self-learner; here "
        "they report the recognised command and the request state."
    )
    icon = "terminal"
    name = "RagCommand"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="command",
            display_name="Command",
            options=["stats", "learn", "bad", "exit"],
            value="stats",
            info="Which cmd_* node of the LangGraph graph this instance stands for.",
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="Message", name="message", method="run_command")]

    NOTES = {
        "stats": "cmd_stats: reports interaction counts, successful answers and learned QA pairs "
                 "(feedback_store.count / count_good, learned_collection.count).",
        "learn": "cmd_learn: runs self_learner.run_distillation() and reports the number of "
                 "distilled QA pairs added to the learned_qa collection.",
        "bad": "cmd_bad: marks the previous interaction USER_THUMBSDOWN and persists the "
               "thumbdown with its variants when the feedback is long enough.",
        "exit": "cmd_exit: terminates the session (raises SystemExit in the LangGraph app).",
    }

    def run_command(self) -> Message:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        note = self.NOTES.get(self.command, "")
        text = "[{0}] {1}\n\nRequest: {2!r}".format(
            self.command, note, state.get("user_input", "")
        )
        self.status = text
        return Message(text=text)
'''


GENERATE_QUERY_VARIANTS = HEADER + r'''
ROLE_AND_RULES = __ROLE_AND_RULES__

PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS = __PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS__


class RagGenerateQueryVariants(Component):
    display_name = "generate_query_variants"
    description = (
        "Expands the user question into short, semantically distinct search-query variants. "
        "The branch budget follows the BRANCH_BUDGET_* rules in config.py; the original query "
        "is always kept as the first variant. Also the retry target of the two graph cycles."
    )
    icon = "git-branch"
    name = "RagGenerateQueryVariants"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Language Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="role_and_rules", display_name="Role and Rules", value=ROLE_AND_RULES),
        MultilineInput(
            name="process_instructions",
            display_name="Process Instructions",
            value=PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS,
        ),
        IntInput(name="max_total_retrievals", display_name="MAX_TOTAL_RETRIEVALS", value=5, advanced=True),
        IntInput(name="budget_short", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_FOR_SHORT_QUESTIONS", value=2, advanced=True),
        IntInput(name="budget_medium", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_FOR_MEDIUM_QUESTIONS", value=2, advanced=True),
        IntInput(
            name="budget_conjunction",
            display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_FOR_CONJUNCTION_QUESTIONS",
            value=3,
            advanced=True,
        ),
        IntInput(name="short_word_count", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_SHORT_WORD_COUNT", value=4, advanced=True),
        IntInput(name="medium_word_count", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_MEDIUM_WORD_COUNT", value=8, advanced=True),
        StrInput(
            name="conjunction_keywords",
            display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_CONJUNCTION_KEYWORDS",
            value="and, or, both, versus, vs, compare, difference, how, why, explain, list, describe, contrast",
            advanced=True,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="build_variants")]

    def _budget(self, query):
        words = query.split()
        budget = self.budget_short if len(words) <= self.short_word_count else self.budget_medium
        keywords = {word.strip().lower() for word in (self.conjunction_keywords or "").split(",") if word.strip()}
        if keywords & {word.lower().strip("?,.") for word in words}:
            budget = max(budget, self.budget_conjunction)
        return max(1, min(budget, self.max_total_retrievals))

    def build_variants(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        query = state.get("query") or state.get("user_input") or ""

        # Re-entry from combine_tracks / check_answer_quality is a retry pass.
        if state.get("query_variants"):
            state["retry_count"] = int(state.get("retry_count", 0)) + 1
            state["blocked_variants"] = list(
                dict.fromkeys(list(state.get("blocked_variants") or []) + list(state.get("query_variants") or []))
            )
            # A retry re-runs retrieval from scratch.
            for key in (
                "retrieved_document_chunks",
                "retrieved_learned_qa_chunks",
                "variants_with_chunks",
                "newly_failed_variants",
            ):
                state[key] = []

        budget = self._budget(query)
        state["branch_budget"] = budget

        if not _switch(state, "ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION") or not query:
            state["query_variants"] = [query] if query else []
            self.status = "sub-query generation disabled -> {0}".format(state["query_variants"])
            return Data(data=state)

        blocked = list(state.get("blocked_variants") or [])
        feedback = [item.get("user_feedback", "") for item in (state.get("prior_thumbdowns") or [])]
        prior_block = ""
        if blocked or feedback:
            prior_block = "PRIOR-FAILURE CONTEXT:\nBlocked variants: {0}\nUser feedback: {1}\n\n".format(
                json.dumps(blocked), json.dumps([f for f in feedback if f])
            )

        prompt = "{0}\n{1}RETRIEVAL BUDGET: {2} variants.\n\nUSER QUESTION:\n{3}\n\n{4}".format(
            self.role_and_rules, prior_block, budget, query, self.process_instructions
        )
        parsed = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), [])

        variants = [query]
        seen = {query.lower()}
        blocked_lower = {item.lower() for item in blocked}
        for item in parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else []:
            candidate = str(item.get("query", "") if isinstance(item, dict) else item).strip()
            key = candidate.lower()
            if not candidate or key in seen or key in blocked_lower:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            variants.append(candidate)
            if len(variants) >= budget:
                break

        state["query_variants"] = variants
        self.status = "budget={0} retry={1} variants={2}".format(
            budget, state.get("retry_count", 0), json.dumps(variants, indent=2)
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


RETRIEVE = HEADER + r'''

class RagRetrieve(Component):
    display_name = "retrieve"
    description = (
        "Queries the Chroma 'documents' and 'learned_qa' collections once per query variant "
        "with a single sentence-transformers embedding each. In LangGraph this node is fanned "
        "out with one Send per variant and the results accumulate through operator.add reducers; "
        "here the same variants are iterated inside the component."
    )
    icon = "search"
    name = "RagRetrieve"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        StrInput(
            name="vector_store_path",
            display_name="VECTOR_STORE_PATH",
            value="data/vector_store",
            info="Chroma PersistentClient path (absolute, or relative to the Langflow working directory).",
        ),
        StrInput(name="documents_collection", display_name="COLLECTION_NAME", value="documents"),
        StrInput(name="learned_collection", display_name="LEARNED_COLLECTION", value="learned_qa"),
        StrInput(
            name="embedding_model",
            display_name="Embedding Model",
            value="all-MiniLM-L6-v2",
            info="sentence-transformers model, matching EmbeddingManager in the LangGraph app.",
        ),
        IntInput(name="top_k", display_name="RETRIEVAL_TOP_K", value=4),
        IntInput(name="top_l", display_name="RETRIEVAL_TOP_L", value=4),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="retrieve")]

    def _query_collection(self, client, name, embedding, n_results):
        try:
            collection = client.get_collection(name=name)
        except Exception:  # collection missing -> empty track
            return []
        result = collection.query(
            query_embeddings=[embedding],
            n_results=n_results,
            include=["documents", "metadatas", "distances"],
        )
        documents = (result.get("documents") or [[]])[0]
        metadatas = (result.get("metadatas") or [[]])[0]
        distances = (result.get("distances") or [[]])[0]
        chunks = []
        for index, content in enumerate(documents):
            metadata = metadatas[index] if index < len(metadatas) else {}
            distance = distances[index] if index < len(distances) else 1.0
            chunks.append(
                {
                    "content": content,
                    "source": (metadata or {}).get("source", name),
                    "metadata": metadata or {},
                    "score": round(1.0 - float(distance), 4),
                    "collection": name,
                }
            )
        return chunks

    def retrieve(self) -> Data:
        import chromadb
        from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

        state = _state_of(self.state)
        variants = list(state.get("query_variants") or [])
        if not variants and state.get("query"):
            variants = [state["query"]]

        documents_chunks = []
        learned_chunks = []
        variants_with_chunks = []
        failed_variants = []

        try:
            client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=self.vector_store_path)
            model = SentenceTransformer(self.embedding_model)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - surfaced in the state, not raised
            state["retrieval_error"] = "{0}: {1}".format(type(exc).__name__, exc)
            state["retrieved_document_chunks"] = []
            state["retrieved_learned_qa_chunks"] = []
            state["variants_with_chunks"] = []
            state["newly_failed_variants"] = variants
            self.status = "retrieval unavailable: " + state["retrieval_error"]
            return Data(data=state)

        for variant in variants:
            embedding = model.encode(
                variant, convert_to_numpy=True, normalize_embeddings=True
            ).tolist()
            variant_documents = self._query_collection(
                client, self.documents_collection, embedding, self.top_k
            )
            variant_learned = self._query_collection(
                client, self.learned_collection, embedding, self.top_l
            )
            for chunk in variant_documents + variant_learned:
                chunk["variant"] = variant
            documents_chunks.extend(variant_documents)
            learned_chunks.extend(variant_learned)
            if variant_documents or variant_learned:
                variants_with_chunks.append(
                    {
                        "query": variant,
                        "document_chunks": variant_documents,
                        "learned_qa_chunks": variant_learned,
                    }
                )
            else:
                failed_variants.append(variant)

        state["retrieved_document_chunks"] = documents_chunks
        state["retrieved_learned_qa_chunks"] = learned_chunks
        state["variants_with_chunks"] = variants_with_chunks
        state["newly_failed_variants"] = failed_variants
        self.status = "variants={0} documents={1} learned_qa={2}".format(
            len(variants), len(documents_chunks), len(learned_chunks)
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


POST_RETRIEVAL_FILTER = HEADER + r'''

class RagPostRetrievalFilter(Component):
    display_name = "post_retrieval_filter"
    description = (
        "Fan-in barrier after every retrieve branch. Drops chunks below the per-track "
        "similarity floor, removes exact duplicates across variants and caps each track "
        "before the two validation branches fan out."
    )
    icon = "filter"
    name = "RagPostRetrievalFilter"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        FloatInput(
            name="documents_min_similarity",
            display_name="DOCUMENTS_MIN_SIMILARITY",
            value=0.53,
        ),
        FloatInput(
            name="learned_qa_min_similarity",
            display_name="LEARNED_QA_MIN_SIMILARITY",
            value=0.57,
        ),
        IntInput(
            name="max_chunks_per_track",
            display_name="Max Chunks per Track",
            value=12,
            advanced=True,
            info="Upper bound kept per track after filtering, highest similarity first.",
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="filter_chunks")]

    def _filter(self, chunks, floor):
        kept = []
        seen = set()
        for chunk in sorted(chunks, key=lambda item: item.get("score", 0.0), reverse=True):
            content = (chunk.get("content") or "").strip()
            key = " ".join(content.lower().split())
            if not content or key in seen:
                continue
            if float(chunk.get("score", 0.0)) < floor:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            kept.append(chunk)
            if len(kept) >= self.max_chunks_per_track:
                break
        return kept

    def filter_chunks(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        documents = self._filter(
            list(state.get("retrieved_document_chunks") or []), self.documents_min_similarity
        )
        learned = self._filter(
            list(state.get("retrieved_learned_qa_chunks") or []), self.learned_qa_min_similarity
        )
        state["post_filtered_document_chunks"] = documents
        state["post_filtered_learned_qa_chunks"] = learned
        self.status = "kept documents={0} learned_qa={1}".format(len(documents), len(learned))
        return Data(data=state)
'''


RETRIEVAL_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT = __RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagRetrievalValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_retrieval"
    description = (
        "Per-track LLM relevance judge over the post-filtered chunks. Keeps only the chunks "
        "the judge marks relevant and records the PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL verdict on the state. "
        "Pass-through when ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_VALIDATION is off."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagRetrievalValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_retrieval_validation",
            display_name="ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_VALIDATION",
            value=True,
        ),
        MultilineInput(
            name="judge_prompt",
            display_name="Retrieval Judge Prompt",
            value=RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = list(state.get("post_filtered_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])

        if not chunks or not self.enable_retrieval_validation:
            state["validated_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
            self.status = "validation skipped ({0} chunks)".format(len(chunks))
            return Data(data=state)

        prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
            query=state.get("query", ""), chunks_block=_chunks_block(chunks)
        )
        verdict = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
        per_chunk = verdict.get("per_chunk") or [] if isinstance(verdict, dict) else []
        relevant = {
            int(item.get("index", -1))
            for item in per_chunk
            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("relevant")
        }
        kept = [chunk for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks) if index in relevant] if per_chunk else chunks

        state["validated_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = kept
        state["{0}_retrieval_verdict".format(field)] = (
            verdict.get("verdict", "UNKNOWN") if isinstance(verdict, dict) else "UNKNOWN"
        )
        self.status = "verdict={0} kept={1}/{2}".format(
            state["{0}_retrieval_verdict".format(field)], len(kept), len(chunks)
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


DEDUP_MERGE = HEADER + r'''
CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT = __CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT__


class RagDedupMerge(Component):
    display_name = "dedup_merge"
    description = (
        "Groups near-duplicate validated chunks (token Jaccard overlap >= "
        "MERGE_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD) and asks the model to consolidate each group into a "
        "single chunk that keeps every fact and cites every source. Singletons pass through."
    )
    icon = "combine"
    name = "RagDedupMerge"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(name="enable_dedup_merge", display_name="ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_DEDUP_MERGE", value=True),
        FloatInput(
            name="merge_similarity_threshold",
            display_name="MERGE_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD",
            value=0.9,
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="merge_prompt", display_name="Chunk Merge Prompt", value=CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="merge")]

    @staticmethod
    def _similarity(left, right):
        left_tokens = set(re.findall(r"\w+", (left or "").lower()))
        right_tokens = set(re.findall(r"\w+", (right or "").lower()))
        if not left_tokens or not right_tokens:
            return 0.0
        return len(left_tokens & right_tokens) / float(len(left_tokens | right_tokens))

    def _groups(self, chunks):
        groups = []
        assigned = set()
        for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
            if index in assigned:
                continue
            group = [index]
            assigned.add(index)
            for other in range(index + 1, len(chunks)):
                if other in assigned:
                    continue
                if self._similarity(chunk.get("content"), chunks[other].get("content")) >= self.merge_similarity_threshold:
                    group.append(other)
                    assigned.add(other)
            groups.append(group)
        return groups

    def merge(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = list(state.get("validated_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])

        if not chunks or not self.enable_dedup_merge:
            state["dedup_merged_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
            state["dedup_merge_{0}_pairs".format(field)] = []
            self.status = "dedup-merge skipped ({0} chunks)".format(len(chunks))
            return Data(data=state)

        merged = []
        pairs = []
        for group in self._groups(chunks):
            members = [chunks[index] for index in group]
            if len(members) == 1:
                merged.append(members[0])
                continue
            prompt = self.merge_prompt.format(chunks_block=_chunks_block(members))
            result = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
            content = (result or {}).get("content") if isinstance(result, dict) else ""
            if not content:
                merged.extend(members)
                continue
            sources = (result.get("sources") or [chunk.get("source", "?") for chunk in members])
            merged_chunk = {
                "content": content,
                "source": ", ".join(dict.fromkeys(str(source) for source in sources)),
                "score": max(float(chunk.get("score", 0.0)) for chunk in members),
                "merged_from": len(members),
            }
            merged.append(merged_chunk)
            pairs.append({"sources": members, "merged": merged_chunk})

        state["dedup_merged_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = merged
        state["dedup_merge_{0}_pairs".format(field)] = pairs
        self.status = "{0} -> {1} chunks ({2} merges)".format(len(chunks), len(merged), len(pairs))
        return Data(data=state)
'''


MERGE_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT = __MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagMergeValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_dedup_merge"
    description = (
        "Faithfulness judge over every merge this track performed. A merged chunk that the "
        "judge reports as fabricating or dropping facts is reverted to its source chunks. "
        "Always present so the downstream compression track has a fixed predecessor."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagMergeValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_dedup_merge_validation",
            display_name="ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_DEDUP_MERGE_VALIDATION",
            value=True,
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="judge_prompt", display_name="Merge Judge Prompt", value=MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = list(state.get("dedup_merged_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])
        pairs = list(state.get("dedup_merge_{0}_pairs".format(field)) or [])

        if not pairs or not self.enable_dedup_merge_validation:
            self.status = "merge validation skipped ({0} merges)".format(len(pairs))
            return Data(data=state)

        reverted = 0
        for pair in pairs:
            merged = pair.get("merged") or {}
            sources = pair.get("sources") or []
            prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
                sources_block=_chunks_block(sources),
                merged_block=merged.get("content", ""),
            )
            verdict = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
            unfaithful = bool(
                isinstance(verdict, dict)
                and (verdict.get("fabricated_claims") or verdict.get("dropped_claims"))
            )
            if not unfaithful:
                continue
            position = next(
                (index for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks) if chunk is merged or chunk == merged), None
            )
            if position is None:
                continue
            chunks[position:position + 1] = sources
            reverted += 1

        state["dedup_merged_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
        state["dedup_merge_{0}_reverted".format(field)] = reverted
        self.status = "{0}/{1} merges reverted".format(reverted, len(pairs))
        return Data(data=state)
'''

NAC = HEADER + r'''
CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT = __CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT__


class RagNacCompress(Component):
    display_name = "NAC_documents"
    description = (
        "Neighbour-Aware Compression: consolidates chunks that come from the same source "
        "document and sit next to each other (consecutive chunk_seq) into one chunk, so the "
        "later stages see whole passages instead of split fragments. Documents track only."
    )
    icon = "layers"
    name = "RagNacCompress"

    ROUTES = ("to_validator", "to_dc")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        BoolInput(name="enable_nac", display_name="ENABLE_NAC_COMPRESSION", value=True),
        MultilineInput(name="merge_prompt", display_name="Chunk Merge Prompt", value=CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Validator", name="to_validator", method="route_validator"),
        Output(display_name="To DC", name="to_dc", method="route_dc"),
    ]

    @staticmethod
    def _neighbour_groups(chunks):
        groups = []
        for chunk in chunks:
            metadata = chunk.get("metadata") or {}
            source = chunk.get("source", "?")
            sequence = metadata.get("chunk_seq", metadata.get("doc_index"))
            placed = False
            if sequence is not None:
                for group in groups:
                    last = group[-1]
                    last_metadata = last.get("metadata") or {}
                    last_sequence = last_metadata.get("chunk_seq", last_metadata.get("doc_index"))
                    if last.get("source") == source and last_sequence is not None:
                        try:
                            if abs(int(sequence) - int(last_sequence)) == 1:
                                group.append(chunk)
                                placed = True
                                break
                        except (TypeError, ValueError):
                            continue
            if not placed:
                groups.append([chunk])
        return groups

    def _run(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        chunks = list(state.get("dedup_merged_document_chunks") or [])
        if not chunks or not self.enable_nac:
            state["nac_output_document_chunks"] = chunks
            state["nac_merge_pairs_documents"] = []
            return state, "NAC skipped ({0} chunks)".format(len(chunks))

        output = []
        pairs = []
        for group in self._neighbour_groups(chunks):
            if len(group) == 1:
                output.append(group[0])
                continue
            prompt = self.merge_prompt.format(chunks_block=_chunks_block(group))
            result = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
            content = result.get("content") if isinstance(result, dict) else ""
            if not content:
                output.extend(group)
                continue
            merged = {
                "content": content,
                "source": group[0].get("source", "?"),
                "score": max(float(chunk.get("score", 0.0)) for chunk in group),
                "merged_from": len(group),
            }
            output.append(merged)
            pairs.append({"sources": group, "merged": merged})

        state["nac_output_document_chunks"] = output
        state["nac_merge_pairs_documents"] = pairs
        return state, "{0} -> {1} chunks ({2} neighbour merges)".format(len(chunks), len(output), len(pairs))

    def _emit(self, target):
        state, status = self._run()
        route = "to_validator" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION") else "to_dc"
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "{0} | route={1}".format(status, route)
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_validator(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_validator")

    def route_dc(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_dc")
'''


NAC_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT = __MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagNacValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_NAC_documents"
    description = (
        "Faithfulness judge over the neighbour merges NAC performed. Any merge the judge "
        "reports as fabricating or dropping facts is reverted to its source chunks."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagNacValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        MultilineInput(name="judge_prompt", display_name="Merge Judge Prompt", value=MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        chunks = list(state.get("nac_output_document_chunks") or [])
        pairs = list(state.get("nac_merge_pairs_documents") or [])
        if not pairs:
            self.status = "nothing to validate"
            return Data(data=state)

        reverted = 0
        for pair in pairs:
            merged = pair.get("merged") or {}
            sources = pair.get("sources") or []
            prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
                sources_block=_chunks_block(sources), merged_block=merged.get("content", "")
            )
            verdict = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
            if not (isinstance(verdict, dict) and (verdict.get("fabricated_claims") or verdict.get("dropped_claims"))):
                continue
            position = next((index for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks) if chunk == merged), None)
            if position is None:
                continue
            chunks[position:position + 1] = sources
            reverted += 1

        state["nac_output_document_chunks"] = chunks
        state["nac_reverted_documents"] = reverted
        self.status = "{0}/{1} neighbour merges reverted".format(reverted, len(pairs))
        return Data(data=state)
'''


DC = HEADER + r'''
DC_SCAN_PROMPT = __DC_SCAN_PROMPT__


class RagDcCompress(Component):
    display_name = "DC_documents"
    description = (
        "Duplicate-Content compression. Slides a DC_WINDOW_SIZE window over the track's "
        "chunks, asks the model which sentences from DIFFERENT chunks state the same fact, "
        "and keeps only the first occurrence of each confirmed redundancy group."
    )
    icon = "scissors"
    name = "RagDcCompress"

    ROUTES = ("to_validator", "to_lbc")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(name="enable_dc", display_name="ENABLE_DC_COMPRESSION", value=True),
        IntInput(name="window_size", display_name="DC_WINDOW_SIZE", value=3),
        MultilineInput(name="scan_prompt", display_name="DC Scan Prompt", value=DC_SCAN_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Validator", name="to_validator", method="route_validator"),
        Output(display_name="To LBC", name="to_lbc", method="route_lbc"),
    ]

    def _input_chunks(self, state, field):
        if self.track == "documents":
            return list(
                state.get("nac_output_document_chunks")
                or state.get("dedup_merged_document_chunks")
                or []
            )
        return list(state.get("dedup_merged_learned_qa_chunks") or [])

    def _run(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = [dict(chunk) for chunk in self._input_chunks(state, field)]

        if len(chunks) < 2 or not self.enable_dc:
            state["dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
            state["dc_groups_per_window_{0}".format(self.track)] = []
            return state, "DC skipped ({0} chunks)".format(len(chunks))

        windows = []
        removed = 0
        window = max(2, self.window_size)
        for start in range(0, len(chunks), window):
            members = chunks[start:start + window]
            if len(members) < 2:
                continue
            prompt = self.scan_prompt.format(chunks_block=_chunks_block(members))
            groups = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), [])
            groups = [group for group in groups if isinstance(group, dict) and group.get("members")]
            windows.append({"window_start": start, "groups": groups})
            for group in groups:
                sentences = [
                    (int(member.get("chunk_index", -1)), str(member.get("sentence", "")).strip())
                    for member in group.get("members", [])
                    if isinstance(member, dict)
                ]
                # keep the first occurrence, delete the rest
                for local_index, sentence in sentences[1:]:
                    if not (0 <= local_index < len(members)) or not sentence:
                        continue
                    target = members[local_index]
                    kept = [line for line in _sentences(target.get("content", "")) if line != sentence]
                    if len(kept) != len(_sentences(target.get("content", ""))):
                        target["content"] = " ".join(kept)
                        removed += 1

        state["dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = [
            chunk for chunk in chunks if (chunk.get("content") or "").strip()
        ]
        state["dc_groups_per_window_{0}".format(self.track)] = windows
        return state, "{0} redundant sentence(s) removed across {1} window(s)".format(removed, len(windows))

    def _emit(self, target):
        state, status = self._run()
        route = "to_validator" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION") else "to_lbc"
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "{0} | route={1}".format(status, route)
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_validator(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_validator")

    def route_lbc(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_lbc")
'''


DC_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
REDUNDANCY_JUDGE_PROMPT = __REDUNDANCY_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagDcValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_DC_documents"
    description = (
        "Strict judge over the redundancy groups DC acted on. Groups it REJECTS are recorded "
        "so the sentences DC removed for them can be restored to their chunk."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagDcValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        MultilineInput(
            name="judge_prompt",
            display_name="Redundancy Judge Prompt",
            value=REDUNDANCY_JUDGE_PROMPT,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = [dict(chunk) for chunk in (state.get("dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])]
        windows = list(state.get("dc_groups_per_window_{0}".format(self.track)) or [])
        if not windows:
            self.status = "nothing to validate"
            return Data(data=state)

        restored = 0
        for entry in windows:
            groups = entry.get("groups") or []
            if not groups:
                continue
            start = int(entry.get("window_start", 0))
            members = chunks[start:start + max(2, len(groups) + 1)] or chunks
            prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
                chunks_block=_chunks_block(members),
                groups_block=json.dumps(groups, indent=2),
            )
            verdicts = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), [])
            rejected = {
                int(item.get("group_index", -1))
                for item in verdicts
                if isinstance(item, dict) and str(item.get("verdict", "")).upper() == "REJECTED"
            }
            for group_index in rejected:
                if not (0 <= group_index < len(groups)):
                    continue
                for member in groups[group_index].get("members", [])[1:]:
                    local_index = int(member.get("chunk_index", -1))
                    sentence = str(member.get("sentence", "")).strip()
                    target_index = start + local_index
                    if not sentence or not (0 <= target_index < len(chunks)):
                        continue
                    content = chunks[target_index].get("content", "")
                    if sentence not in content:
                        chunks[target_index]["content"] = (content + " " + sentence).strip()
                        restored += 1

        state["dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
        state["dc_restored_{0}".format(self.track)] = restored
        self.status = "{0} sentence(s) restored after rejected groups".format(restored)
        return Data(data=state)
'''


LBC = HEADER + r'''
LBC_COMPRESS_PROMPT = __LBC_COMPRESS_PROMPT__

LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS = __LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS__


class RagLbcCompress(Component):
    display_name = "LBC_documents"
    description = (
        "Query-focused Line-By-line Compression. Rewrites each chunk keeping only the "
        "sentences that help answer the query; chunks the model reports as __IRRELEVANT__ "
        "are dropped, and a compression that falls under LBC_MIN_RETENTION_RATIO is refused."
    )
    icon = "minimize-2"
    name = "RagLbcCompress"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(name="enable_lbc", display_name="ENABLE_LBC_COMPRESSION", value=True),
        FloatInput(
            name="min_retention_ratio",
            display_name="LBC_MIN_RETENTION_RATIO",
            value=0.35,
            info="A compressed chunk shorter than this fraction of the original is rejected.",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="compress_prompt", display_name="LBC Compress Prompt", value=LBC_COMPRESS_PROMPT),
        MultilineInput(
            name="instructions",
            display_name="LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS",
            value=LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="compress")]

    def compress(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = list(state.get("dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])
        query = state.get("query", "")

        if not chunks or not self.enable_lbc:
            state["compressed_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
            state["lbc_validation_pairs_{0}".format(self.track)] = []
            self.status = "LBC skipped ({0} chunks)".format(len(chunks))
            return Data(data=state)

        compressed = []
        pairs = []
        dropped = 0
        for chunk in chunks:
            original = chunk.get("content", "")
            prompt = self.compress_prompt.format(
                query=query,
                instructions=self.instructions,
                source=chunk.get("source", "?"),
                content=original,
            )
            result = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
            text = str((result or {}).get("compressed", "")).strip() if isinstance(result, dict) else ""
            if text == "__IRRELEVANT__":
                dropped += 1
                continue
            if not text or len(text) < self.min_retention_ratio * max(1, len(original)):
                # under-retention: keep the original chunk rather than lose facts
                compressed.append(chunk)
                continue
            new_chunk = dict(chunk)
            new_chunk["content"] = text
            new_chunk["compressed_from"] = original
            compressed.append(new_chunk)
            pairs.append({"original": chunk, "compressed": new_chunk})

        state["compressed_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = compressed
        state["lbc_validation_pairs_{0}".format(self.track)] = pairs
        self.status = "{0} -> {1} chunks ({2} dropped as irrelevant)".format(
            len(chunks), len(compressed), dropped
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


LBC_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
LBC_JUDGE_PROMPT = __LBC_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagLbcValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_LBC_documents"
    description = (
        "Compression-safety judge for LBC. A chunk judged OVER_COMPRESSED or FABRICATED is "
        "restored to its pre-compression content. Always present (pass-through when "
        "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION is off) so combine_tracks has a fixed predecessor."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagLbcValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="judge_prompt", display_name="LBC Judge Prompt", value=LBC_JUDGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = list(state.get("compressed_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])
        pairs = list(state.get("lbc_validation_pairs_{0}".format(self.track)) or [])

        if not pairs or not _switch(state, "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION"):
            self.status = "LBC validation skipped ({0} compressions)".format(len(pairs))
            return Data(data=state)

        restored = 0
        for pair in pairs:
            original = pair.get("original") or {}
            compressed = pair.get("compressed") or {}
            prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
                query=state.get("query", ""),
                source=original.get("source", "?"),
                original_content=original.get("content", ""),
                compressed_content=compressed.get("content", ""),
            )
            verdict = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
            label = str((verdict or {}).get("verdict", "UNKNOWN")).upper() if isinstance(verdict, dict) else "UNKNOWN"
            if label in {"SAFE", "UNKNOWN"}:
                continue
            position = next((index for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks) if chunk == compressed), None)
            if position is None:
                continue
            chunks[position] = original
            restored += 1

        state["compressed_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
        state["lbc_restored_{0}".format(self.track)] = restored
        self.status = "{0}/{1} chunks restored".format(restored, len(pairs))
        return Data(data=state)
'''


COMBINE_TRACKS = HEADER + r'''

class RagCombineTracks(Component):
    display_name = "combine_tracks"
    description = (
        "Barrier after both compression pipelines. Merges the documents-track and "
        "learned-QA-track states, builds compressed_docs, then routes exactly as "
        "route_after_combine: draft, direct answer, retry, or no_context_answer."
    )
    icon = "merge"
    name = "RagCombineTracks"

    ROUTES = ("to_draft", "to_answer", "to_retry", "to_no_context")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="documents_state", display_name="Documents Track", is_list=True),
        DataInput(name="learned_qa_state", display_name="Learned-QA Track", is_list=True),
        IntInput(name="retry_limit", display_name="LLM_RESPONSE_RETRY_LIMIT", value=3, advanced=True),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Draft", name="to_draft", method="route_draft"),
        Output(display_name="To Answer", name="to_answer", method="route_answer"),
        Output(display_name="Retry", name="to_retry", method="route_retry"),
        Output(display_name="No Context", name="to_no_context", method="route_no_context"),
    ]

    def _combined(self):
        documents_state = _state_of(self.documents_state)
        learned_state = _state_of(self.learned_qa_state)
        state = dict(documents_state) or dict(learned_state)
        for key, value in learned_state.items():
            if key not in state or "learned_qa" in key:
                state[key] = value
        state["compressed_docs"] = list(state.get("compressed_document_chunks") or []) + list(
            state.get("compressed_learned_qa_chunks") or []
        )
        return state

    def _route(self, state):
        limit = int(state.get("retry_limit", self.retry_limit) or self.retry_limit)
        if not state.get("compressed_docs"):
            if int(state.get("retry_count", 0)) >= limit:
                return "to_no_context"
            return "to_retry"
        return "to_draft" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_ANSWER_DRAFT_CREATION") else "to_answer"

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._combined()
        route = self._route(state)
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "compressed_docs={0} retry_count={1} route={2}".format(
            len(state.get("compressed_docs") or []), state.get("retry_count", 0), route
        )
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_draft(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_draft")

    def route_answer(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_answer")

    def route_retry(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_retry")

    def route_no_context(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_no_context")
'''


GENERATE_DRAFT = HEADER + r'''
GENERATE_DRAFT_PROMPT = __GENERATE_DRAFT_PROMPT__


class RagGenerateDraft(Component):
    display_name = "generate_draft"
    description = (
        "Writes a grounded working answer from compressed_docs, then routes to the quality "
        "check or straight to generate_answer depending on ENABLE_ANSWER_QUALITY_CHECK."
    )
    icon = "pencil-line"
    name = "RagGenerateDraft"

    ROUTES = ("to_quality", "to_answer")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        MultilineInput(name="draft_prompt", display_name="Draft Prompt", value=GENERATE_DRAFT_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Quality Check", name="to_quality", method="route_quality"),
        Output(display_name="To Answer", name="to_answer", method="route_answer"),
    ]

    def _run(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if state.get("draft"):
            return state
        context = _chunks_block(state.get("compressed_docs") or [])
        prompt = self.draft_prompt.format(query=state.get("query", ""), context=context)
        state["draft"] = _llm_text(self.llm, prompt)
        return state

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._run()
        route = "to_quality" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_ANSWER_QUALITY_CHECK") else "to_answer"
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = state.get("draft", "")
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_quality(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_quality")

    def route_answer(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_answer")
'''


CHECK_ANSWER_QUALITY = HEADER + r'''
GROUNDING_PROMPT = __GROUNDING_PROMPT__


class RagCheckAnswerQuality(Component):
    display_name = "check_answer_quality"
    description = (
        "Grounding judge over the draft. GROUNDED continues to generate_answer; any other "
        "verdict retries retrieval while ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION is on and the retry "
        "budget lasts, otherwise the best-effort draft is used. Mirrors route_after_quality_check."
    )
    icon = "scale"
    name = "RagCheckAnswerQuality"

    ROUTES = ("to_answer", "to_retry")
    PASS_VERDICT = "GROUNDED"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        MultilineInput(name="grounding_prompt", display_name="Grounding Prompt", value=GROUNDING_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Answer", name="to_answer", method="route_answer"),
        Output(display_name="Retry", name="to_retry", method="route_retry"),
    ]

    def _run(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if state.get("quality_verdict"):
            return state
        prompt = self.grounding_prompt.format(
            query=state.get("query", ""),
            context=_chunks_block(state.get("compressed_docs") or []),
            answer=state.get("draft", ""),
        )
        verdict = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {})
        state["quality_verdict"] = (
            str(verdict.get("verdict", "UNKNOWN")).upper() if isinstance(verdict, dict) else "UNKNOWN"
        )
        state["quality_reason"] = verdict.get("overall_reason", "") if isinstance(verdict, dict) else ""
        return state

    def _route(self, state):
        if state.get("quality_verdict") == self.PASS_VERDICT:
            return "to_answer"
        limit = int(state.get("retry_limit", 3) or 3)
        budget_ok = _switch(state, "ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION") and int(state.get("retry_count", 0)) < limit
        return "to_retry" if budget_ok else "to_answer"

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._run()
        route = self._route(state)
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "verdict={0} route={1} reason={2}".format(
            state.get("quality_verdict"), route, state.get("quality_reason", "")
        )
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_answer(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_answer")

    def route_retry(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_retry")
'''


GENERATE_ANSWER = HEADER + r'''
GENERATE_ANSWER_PROMPT = __GENERATE_ANSWER_PROMPT__

GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT = __GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT__


class RagGenerateAnswer(Component):
    description = (
        "Final answer node. Uses the draft as working material when one exists, otherwise "
        "answers directly from compressed_docs. Always emits the answer message; the "
        "distillation branch is taken only when ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION is on."
    )
    display_name = "generate_answer"
    icon = "message-square"
    name = "RagGenerateAnswer"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        MultilineInput(name="answer_prompt", display_name="Answer Prompt", value=GENERATE_ANSWER_PROMPT),
        MultilineInput(
            name="answer_from_draft_prompt",
            display_name="Answer From Draft Prompt",
            value=GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="Answer", name="answer", method="build_answer"),
        Output(display_name="To Distillation", name="to_distill", method="route_distillation"),
    ]

    def _run(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if state.get("answer"):
            return state
        context = _chunks_block(state.get("compressed_docs") or [])
        if state.get("draft"):
            prompt = self.answer_from_draft_prompt.format(
                query=state.get("query", ""), context=context, draft=state["draft"]
            )
        else:
            prompt = self.answer_prompt.format(query=state.get("query", ""), context=context)
        state["answer"] = _llm_text(self.llm, prompt)
        return state

    def build_answer(self) -> Message:
        state = self._run()
        self._answer_state = state
        self.status = state.get("answer", "")
        return Message(text=state.get("answer", ""))

    def route_distillation(self) -> Data:
        state = getattr(self, "_answer_state", None) or self._run()
        if not _switch(state, "ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION", default=False):
            self.stop("to_distill")
            return Data(data={})
        return Data(data=state)
'''


AUTO_DISTILLATION = HEADER + r'''
DISTILL_PROMPT = __DISTILL_PROMPT__


class RagAutoDistillation(Component):
    display_name = "auto_distillation"
    description = (
        "Distils the verified answer into 1-3 self-contained QA pairs for the learned_qa "
        "collection. In the LangGraph app the pairs are written to Chroma by the self-learner; "
        "here they are reported so the run can be inspected."
    )
    icon = "graduation-cap"
    name = "RagAutoDistillation"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),
        MultilineInput(name="distill_prompt", display_name="Distill Prompt", value=DISTILL_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="QA Pairs", name="qa_pairs", method="distill")]

    def distill(self) -> Message:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if not state:
            return Message(text="")
        prompt = self.distill_prompt.format(
            query=state.get("query", ""),
            answer=state.get("answer", ""),
            chunks=_chunks_block(state.get("compressed_docs") or []),
        )
        pairs = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), [])
        text = json.dumps(pairs, indent=2)
        self.status = text
        return Message(text=text)
'''

class RagNoContextAnswer(Component):
    display_name = "no_context_answer"
    description = (
        "Terminal branch taken when every retrieval retry has been spent and compression "
        "still produced nothing. Returns the fixed no-context message instead of guessing."

    icon = "circle-slash"
    name = "RagNoContextAnswer"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        MultilineInput(
            name="no_context_message",
            display_name="NO_CONTEXT_ANSWER",
            value=NO_CONTEXT_ANSWER,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="Answer", name="answer", method="build_answer")]

    def build_answer(self) -> Message:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        state["answer"] = self.no_context_message
        self.status = self.no_context_message
        return Message(text=self.no_context_message)

PROMPT_TOKENS = {
    "__ROLE_AND_RULES__": P.ROLE_AND_RULES,
    "__PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS__": P.PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS,
    "__RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT__": P.RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT,

    "__GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT__": P.GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT,
    "__DISTILL_PROMPT__": P.DISTILL_PROMPT,
    "__NO_CONTEXT_ANSWER__": P.NO_CONTEXT_ANSWER,
}


def inject(code: str) -> str:
    for token, value in PROMPT_TOKENS.items():
        if token in code:
            code = code.replace(token, repr(value))
    return code


COMPONENT_SOURCES = {
    "RagUserInput": A.USER_INPUT,
    "RagCommand": A.COMMAND,
    "RagGenerateQueryVariants": A.GENERATE_QUERY_VARIANTS,

TEMPLATES: dict[str, dict] = {}
for comp_name, source in COMPONENT_SOURCES.items():
    code = inject(source)
    frontend_node, _instance = build_custom_component_template(Component(_code=code))
    TEMPLATES[comp_name] = frontend_node
    print("built", comp_name)


# ── flow assembly ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
nodes: list[dict] = []
by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}

X = 460
Y = 300


def place(layer: int, row: float) -> dict:
    return {"x": 120 + layer * X, "y": 300 + row * Y}


def add_custom(node_id: str, comp_name: str, layer: int, row: float, *, display_name=None, values=None):
    frontend_node = copy.deepcopy(TEMPLATES[comp_name])
    if display_name:
        frontend_node["display_name"] = display_name
    for field, value in (values or {}).items():
        frontend_node["template"][field]["value"] = value
    node = {
        "data": {
            "description": frontend_node.get("description", ""),
            "display_name": frontend_node.get("display_name", comp_name),
            "id": node_id,
            "node": frontend_node,
            "selected_output": frontend_node["outputs"][0]["name"],
            "showNode": True,
            "type": comp_name,
        },
        "dragging": False,
        "id": node_id,
        "measured": {"height": 420, "width": 320},
        "position": place(layer, row),
        "selected": False,
        "type": "genericNode",
    }
    nodes.append(node)
    by_id[node_id] = node
    return node


# Built-in nodes are copied from the existing Basic LLM Prompting export so their
# templates match this Langflow version exactly.
template_flow = json.loads(TEMPLATE_FLOW.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
BUILTIN = {n["data"]["type"]: n for n in template_flow["data"]["nodes"] if n.get("type") == "genericNode"}


def add_builtin(node_id: str, builtin_type: str, layer: int, row: float, *, display_name=None, values=None):
    node = copy.deepcopy(BUILTIN[builtin_type])
    node["id"] = node_id
    node["data"]["id"] = node_id
    node["position"] = place(layer, row)
    node["positionAbsolute"] = dict(node["position"])
    node.pop("height", None)
    node.pop("width", None)
    if display_name:
        node["data"]["display_name"] = display_name
        node["data"]["node"]["display_name"] = display_name
    for field, value in (values or {}).items():
        node["data"]["node"]["template"][field]["value"] = value
    nodes.append(node)
    by_id[node_id] = node
    return node

# ── edges ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
edges: list[dict] = []


def _encode(payload: dict) -> str:
    return json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True).replace('"', "\u0153")


def add_edge(source_id: str, output_name: str, target_id: str, field_name: str):
    source_node = by_id[source_id]["data"]
    target_node = by_id[target_id]["data"]

    output = next(o for o in source_node["node"]["outputs"] if o["name"] == output_name)
    field = target_node["node"]["template"][field_name]

    source_handle = {
        "dataType": source_node["type"],
        "id": source_id,
        "name": output_name,
        "output_types": list(output["types"]),
    }
    target_handle = {
        "fieldName": field_name,
        "id": target_id,
        "inputTypes": list(field.get("input_types") or []),
        "type": field.get("type", "str"),
    }
    source_encoded = _encode(source_handle)
    target_encoded = _encode(target_handle)
    edges.append(
        {
            "animated": False,
            "className": "",
            "data": {"sourceHandle": source_handle, "targetHandle": target_handle},
            "id": f"xy-edge__{source_id}{source_encoded}-{target_id}{target_encoded}",
            "selected": False,
            "source": source_id,
            "sourceHandle": source_encoded,
            "target": target_id,
            "targetHandle": target_encoded,
        }
    )


add_edge(CHAT_IN, "message", "RagUserInput-rag00", "chat_message")

for command in ("stats", "learn", "bad", "exit"):
    add_edge("RagUserInput-rag00", f"to_{command}", f"RagCommand-{command}", "state")
    add_edge(f"RagCommand-{command}", "message", f"ChatOutput-{command}", "input_value")

add_edge("RagUserInput-rag00", "to_query", "RagGenerateQueryVariants-rag00", "state")
add_edge("RagGenerateQueryVariants-rag00", "state_out", "RagRetrieve-rag00", "state")
add_edge("RagRetrieve-rag00", "state_out", "RagPostRetrievalFilter-rag00", "state")
add_edge("RagPostRetrievalFilter-rag00", "state_out", "RagRetrievalValidator-documents", "state")
add_edge("RagPostRetrievalFilter-rag00", "state_out", "RagRetrievalValidator-learnedqa", "state")
add_edge("RagRetrievalValidator-documents", "state_out", "RagDedupMerge-documents", "state")
add_edge("RagRetrievalValidator-learnedqa", "state_out", "RagDedupMerge-learnedqa", "state")
add_edge("RagDedupMerge-documents", "state_out", "RagMergeValidator-documents", "state")
add_edge("RagDedupMerge-learnedqa", "state_out", "RagMergeValidator-learnedqa", "state")

# documents compression track

# learned-QA compression track
add_edge("RagMergeValidator-learnedqa", "state_out", "RagDcCompress-learnedqa", "state")

CYCLE_EDGES = [
    ("RagCombineTracks-rag00", "to_retry"),

]
if "--no-cycles" not in sys.argv:

        add_edge(source_id, output_name, "RagGenerateQueryVariants-rag00", "state")

# ── notes ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def note(node_id: str, text: str, layer: float, row: float, width=380, height=460, colour="neutral"):
    nodes.append(
        {
            "data": {
                "id": node_id,
                "node": {
                    "description": text,
                    "display_name": "",
                    "documentation": "",
                    "template": {"backgroundColor": colour},
                },
                "type": "note",
            },
            "dragging": False,
            "height": height,
            "id": node_id,
            "measured": {"height": height, "width": width},
            "position": {"x": 120 + layer * X, "y": 300 + row * Y},
            "selected": False,
            "style": {"height": height, "width": width},
            "type": "noteNode",
            "width": width,
        }
    )


note(
    "note-readme",
    "# 📖 LangGraph RAG pipeline, as a Langflow flow\n"
    "Each node here is a Custom Component standing for one node of the LangGraph "
    "`build_graph()` in `graph.py`, wired in the same order. A single `Data` payload carries "
    "the whole `GraphState` from node to node, exactly like LangGraph's state dict.\n\n"
    "## Quick start\n"
    "1. Put your **Groq API key** in the shared *Groq* component (its `Language Model` output "
    "feeds every LLM node, so one key configures the whole flow).\n"
    "2. Point **retrieve** at your Chroma store: `VECTOR_STORE_PATH`, `COLLECTION_NAME`, "
    "`LEARNED_COLLECTION`. It embeds with sentence-transformers `all-MiniLM-L6-v2`, the same "
    "model as `EmbeddingManager`.\n"
    "3. Open the **Playground** and ask a question. A bare `stats` / `learn` / `bad` / `exit` "
    "message takes the command branch instead.\n\n"
    "## How the graph maps\n"
    "* **Conditional edges** (`routes.py`) are components with several outputs that call "
    "`self.stop()` on the branches not taken.\n"
    "* **Parallel Sends** in `retrieve` become one component that loops over the variants — "
    "Langflow fans out on edges, not on payloads.\n"
    "* **`defer=True` on `combine_tracks`** becomes a component with one input per track, so it "
    "only runs once both compression pipelines have delivered.\n"
    "* The **ENABLE_* switches** live on the nodes that read them; the five that drive routing "
    "sit on `user_input` and travel in the state.",
    -0.95,
    -0.9,
    width=400,
    height=760,
)

note(
    "note-cycles",
    "### Retry cycles\n"
    "`combine_tracks → retry` and `check_answer_quality → retry` are the graph's two back-edges "
    "to `generate_query_variants`. Langflow only guarantees bounded cyclic execution through the "
    "`Loop` component (see docs/Research.md topic 1, ADR-005), so if this Langflow build refuses "
    "the cycle, delete these two edges — every other path still runs, and each retry branch is a "
    "no-op when it is not taken.",
    14.0,
    -0.9,
    width=380,
    height=300,
    colour="transparent",
)

note(
    "note-stubs",
    "### What is not re-implemented\n"
    "`nodes.py` from the LangGraph project was not available when this flow was generated, so "
    "the node bodies here are reconstructions from `graph.py`, `routes.py`, `state.py`, "
    "`config.py` and `prompts.py`:\n\n"
    "* Prompts and thresholds are the real ones and are editable on each component.\n"
    "* Near-duplicate detection in `dedup_merge` uses token overlap, not embeddings.\n"
    "* `NAC` merges chunks that are adjacent in the same source document.\n"
    "* The `cmd_*` branches report the command instead of touching MongoDB / the self-learner.\n"
    "* Retrieval reads Chroma directly; there is no post-run feedback write-back.",
    8.0,
    -0.9,
    width=380,
    height=340,
    colour="transparent",
)


flow = {
    "data": {
        "edges": edges,
        "nodes": nodes,
        "viewport": {"x": 0, "y": 0, "zoom": 0.35},
    },
    "description": (
        "The LangGraph RAG pipeline rebuilt in Langflow: query-variant fan-out, Chroma "
        "retrieval over documents + learned QA, per-track validation, dedup-merge and "
        "NAC/DC/LBC compression, then draft, grounding check and final answer."
    ),
    "endpoint_name": None,
    "id": FLOW_ID,
    "is_component": False,
    "last_tested_version": "1.10.2",
    "locked": False,
    "name": "LangGraph RAG Pipeline",
    "tags": ["rag"],
}

OUTPUT.write_text(json.dumps(flow, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
print(f"\nwrote {OUTPUT} ({OUTPUT.stat().st_size / 1024:.0f} KB)")
print(f"nodes={len([n for n in nodes if n['type'] == 'genericNode'])} notes="
      f"{len([n for n in nodes if n['type'] == 'noteNode'])} edges={len(edges)}")
