"""Run every generated component's Python end to end with a fake LLM.

This exercises the real node bodies (prompt .format() keys, state keys, routing)
without needing a Groq key or a Chroma store.
"""

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from gen_flow import COMPONENT_SOURCES, inject  # noqa: E402  (also regenerates the flow)
from lfx.schema.data import Data
from lfx.schema.message import Message


class FakeResponse:
    def __init__(self, content):
        self.content = content


class FakeLLM:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []

    def invoke(self, prompt):
        self.calls.append(prompt)
        lowered = prompt.lower()
        if "query-reformulation" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('[{"query": "asd motor speed"}, {"query": "variable frequency drive"}]')
        if "relevance judge for a retrieval system" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse(
                '{"verdict": "PASS", "per_chunk": [{"index": 0, "relevant": true, "reason": "x"},'
                ' {"index": 1, "relevant": true, "reason": "y"}], "overall_reason": "ok"}'
            )
        if "you merge near-duplicate" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('{"content": "merged text.", "sources": ["a.pdf", "b.pdf"], "merged_from": 2}')
        if "faithfulness judge" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('{"fabricated_claims": [], "dropped_claims": [], "overall_reason": "faithful"}')
        if "sentence-level redundancy annotation" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse(
                '[{"members": [{"chunk_index": 0, "sentence": "Shared fact one."},'
                ' {"chunk_index": 1, "sentence": "Shared fact one."}]}]'
            )
        if "validating proposed redundancy groups" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('[{"group_index": 0, "verdict": "CONFIRMED", "reason": "same fact"}]')
        if "query-focused chunk compressor" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('{"compressed": "Kept sentence one. Kept sentence two.", "dropped_count": 1, "reason": "r"}')
        if "compression-safety judge" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('{"verdict": "SAFE", "fabricated_claims": [], "lost_relevant_facts": [], "overall_reason": "ok"}')
        if "strict quality judge" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('{"verdict": "GROUNDED", "unsupported_claims": [], "scope_mismatch": "", "overall_reason": "ok"}')
        if "knowledge distillation assistant" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('[{"question": "q1", "answer": "a1"}]')
        return FakeResponse("A short grounded answer. [Source: a.pdf]")


CLASSES = {}
for name, source in COMPONENT_SOURCES.items():
    namespace = {}
    exec(inject(source), namespace)  # noqa: S102 - this is the code Langflow will execute
    CLASSES[name] = next(
        value
        for value in namespace.values()
        if isinstance(value, type) and getattr(value, "name", None) == name
    )


def make(name, **overrides):
    """Instantiate a component with its declared defaults, then apply overrides."""
    cls = CLASSES[name]
    instance = cls()
    stopped = []
    instance.stop = stopped.append
    instance._stopped = stopped
    for field in cls.inputs:
        object.__setattr__(instance, field.name, getattr(field, "value", None))
    for key, value in overrides.items():
        object.__setattr__(instance, key, value)
    return instance


def state_of(result):
    return dict(result.data)


failures = []


def check(label, condition, detail=""):
    status = "ok " if condition else "FAIL"
    print(f"  [{status}] {label}{(' - ' + detail) if detail and not condition else ''}")
    if not condition:
        failures.append(label)


llm = FakeLLM()
chunks = [
    {"content": "Shared fact one. ASDs vary motor speed.", "source": "a.pdf",
     "score": 0.81, "metadata": {"source": "a.pdf", "chunk_seq": 0}},
    {"content": "Shared fact one. Drives lower electricity costs.", "source": "a.pdf",
     "score": 0.77, "metadata": {"source": "a.pdf", "chunk_seq": 1}},
    {"content": "An unrelated paragraph about invoices.", "source": "b.pdf",
     "score": 0.60, "metadata": {"source": "b.pdf", "chunk_seq": 7}},
]

print("\n== user_input ==")
node = make("RagUserInput", chat_message=Message(text="How do ASDs control motor speed?"))
query_state = state_of(node.route_query())
check("query branch carries state", bool(query_state.get("query")))
check("command branches stopped", set(node._stopped) == {"to_stats", "to_learn", "to_bad", "to_exit"},
      str(node._stopped))
check("switches resolved", query_state["switches"]["ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION"] is True)

cmd_node = make("RagUserInput", chat_message=Message(text="stats"))
check("stats routes to cmd_stats", bool(state_of(cmd_node.route_stats())))
cmd_node2 = make("RagUserInput", chat_message=Message(text="stats"))
check("stats does not route to query", state_of(cmd_node2.route_query()) == {})

print("\n== cmd_* ==")
node = make("RagCommand", state=Data(data=query_state), command="stats")
check("cmd emits a message", "cmd_stats" not in "" and bool(node.run_command().text))

print("\n== generate_query_variants ==")
node = make("RagGenerateQueryVariants", state=Data(data=query_state), llm=llm)
variant_state = state_of(node.build_variants())
check("original query kept first", variant_state["query_variants"][0] == query_state["query"])
check("variants generated", len(variant_state["query_variants"]) > 1, str(variant_state["query_variants"]))
check("budget recorded", variant_state["branch_budget"] >= 1)

retry_seed = dict(variant_state)
node = make("RagGenerateQueryVariants", state=Data(data=retry_seed), llm=llm)
retry_state = state_of(node.build_variants())
check("retry increments retry_count", retry_state["retry_count"] == 1)
check("previous variants blocked", len(retry_state["blocked_variants"]) > 0)

print("\n== retrieve (no store -> graceful) ==")
node = make("RagRetrieve", state=Data(data=variant_state), vector_store_path="does/not/exist")
retrieved = state_of(node.retrieve())
check("retrieve returns state", "retrieved_document_chunks" in retrieved)

print("\n== post_retrieval_filter ==")
seeded = dict(variant_state)
seeded["retrieved_document_chunks"] = chunks
seeded["retrieved_learned_qa_chunks"] = [
    {"content": "Q: what is an ASD? A: an adjustable speed drive.", "source": "learned_qa", "score": 0.71}
]
node = make("RagPostRetrievalFilter", state=Data(data=seeded))
filtered = state_of(node.filter_chunks())
check("documents filtered by floor", len(filtered["post_filtered_document_chunks"]) == 2,
      str(len(filtered["post_filtered_document_chunks"])))
check("learned_qa kept", len(filtered["post_filtered_learned_qa_chunks"]) == 1)

print("\n== validate_*_retrieval ==")
node = make("RagRetrievalValidator", state=Data(data=filtered), llm=llm, track="documents")
validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("validated_document_chunks set", len(validated["validated_document_chunks"]) == 2)
check("verdict recorded", validated["document_retrieval_verdict"] == "PASS")

node = make("RagRetrievalValidator", state=Data(data=validated), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("validated_learned_qa_chunks set", len(validated["validated_learned_qa_chunks"]) == 1)

print("\n== dedup_merge ==")
node = make("RagDedupMerge", state=Data(data=validated), llm=llm, track="documents")
merged = state_of(node.merge())
check("dedup_merged_document_chunks set", len(merged["dedup_merged_document_chunks"]) >= 1)
node = make("RagDedupMerge", state=Data(data=merged), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
merged = state_of(node.merge())
check("dedup_merged_learned_qa_chunks set", len(merged["dedup_merged_learned_qa_chunks"]) == 1)

print("\n== validate_dedup_merge ==")
node = make("RagMergeValidator", state=Data(data=merged), llm=llm, track="documents")
merge_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("merge validator returns state", "dedup_merged_document_chunks" in merge_validated)
node = make("RagMergeValidator", state=Data(data=merge_validated), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
merge_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("learned_qa merge validator ok", "dedup_merged_learned_qa_chunks" in merge_validated)

print("\n== NAC_documents ==")
node = make("RagNacCompress", state=Data(data=merge_validated), llm=llm)
nac = state_of(node.route_validator())
check("nac output set", "nac_output_document_chunks" in nac)
check("nac routed to validator", node._stopped == ["to_dc"], str(node._stopped))
node = make("RagNacCompress", state=Data(data=merge_validated), llm=llm)
check("nac to_dc stopped when validation on", state_of(node.route_dc()) == {})

print("\n== validate_NAC_documents ==")
node = make("RagNacValidator", state=Data(data=nac), llm=llm)
nac_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("nac validator returns state", "nac_output_document_chunks" in nac_validated)

print("\n== DC ==")
node = make("RagDcCompress", state=Data(data=nac_validated), llm=llm, track="documents")
dc = state_of(node.route_validator())
check("dc output set", "dc_output_document_chunks" in dc)
check("dc window groups recorded", "dc_groups_per_window_documents" in dc)
node = make("RagDcCompress", state=Data(data=dc), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
dc = state_of(node.route_validator())
check("dc learned_qa output set", "dc_output_learned_qa_chunks" in dc)

print("\n== validate_DC ==")
node = make("RagDcValidator", state=Data(data=dc), llm=llm, track="documents")
dc_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("dc validator returns state", "dc_output_document_chunks" in dc_validated)

print("\n== LBC ==")
node = make("RagLbcCompress", state=Data(data=dc_validated), llm=llm, track="documents")
lbc = state_of(node.compress())
check("compressed_document_chunks set", "compressed_document_chunks" in lbc)
node = make("RagLbcCompress", state=Data(data=lbc), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
lbc = state_of(node.compress())
check("compressed_learned_qa_chunks set", "compressed_learned_qa_chunks" in lbc)

print("\n== validate_LBC ==")
node = make("RagLbcValidator", state=Data(data=lbc), llm=llm, track="documents")
lbc_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("lbc validator returns state", "compressed_document_chunks" in lbc_validated)

print("\n== combine_tracks ==")
node = make(
    "RagCombineTracks",
    documents_state=[Data(data=lbc_validated)],
    learned_qa_state=[Data(data=lbc_validated)],
)
combined = state_of(node.route_draft())
check("compressed_docs built", len(combined["compressed_docs"]) > 0, str(combined.get("compressed_docs")))
check("draft branch taken", set(node._stopped) == {"to_answer", "to_retry", "to_no_context"}, str(node._stopped))

empty = dict(lbc_validated)
empty["compressed_document_chunks"] = []
empty["compressed_learned_qa_chunks"] = []
node = make("RagCombineTracks", documents_state=[Data(data=empty)], learned_qa_state=[Data(data=empty)])
check("empty context retries", bool(state_of(node.route_retry())))

exhausted = dict(empty)
exhausted["retry_count"] = 3
node = make("RagCombineTracks", documents_state=[Data(data=exhausted)], learned_qa_state=[Data(data=exhausted)])
check("exhausted retries -> no_context", bool(state_of(node.route_no_context())))

print("\n== generate_draft ==")
node = make("RagGenerateDraft", state=Data(data=combined), llm=llm)
drafted = state_of(node.route_quality())
check("draft written", bool(drafted.get("draft")))

print("\n== check_answer_quality ==")
node = make("RagCheckAnswerQuality", state=Data(data=drafted), llm=llm)
checked = state_of(node.route_answer())
check("verdict recorded", checked["quality_verdict"] == "GROUNDED")
check("retry branch stopped", node._stopped == ["to_retry"], str(node._stopped))

failing = dict(drafted)
node = make("RagCheckAnswerQuality", state=Data(data=failing), llm=FakeLLM())
node.llm.invoke = lambda prompt: FakeResponse('{"verdict": "OVERCLAIMED", "unsupported_claims": [], "scope_mismatch": "", "overall_reason": "too broad"}')
check("bad verdict retries", bool(state_of(node.route_retry())))

print("\n== generate_answer ==")
node = make("RagGenerateAnswer", state=Data(data=checked), llm=llm)
answer = node.build_answer()
check("answer message produced", bool(answer.text))
check("distillation branch stopped by default", state_of(node.route_distillation()) == {})

distil_state = dict(checked)
distil_state["switches"] = dict(distil_state["switches"], ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION=True)
node = make("RagGenerateAnswer", state=Data(data=distil_state), llm=llm)
node.build_answer()
check("distillation branch taken when enabled", bool(state_of(node.route_distillation())))

print("\n== auto_distillation ==")
node = make("RagAutoDistillation", state=Data(data=distil_state), llm=llm)
check("qa pairs emitted", json.loads(node.distill().text)[0]["question"] == "q1")

print("\n== no_context_answer ==")
node = make("RagNoContextAnswer", state=Data(data=exhausted))
check("no-context message emitted", "knowledge base does not contain" in node.build_answer().text)

print("\n" + ("ALL CHECKS PASSED" if not failures else f"{len(failures)} FAILURES: {failures}"))
sys.exit(1 if failures else 0)

import json
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('flows/LangGraph RAG Pipeline.json')
flow = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
nodes = flow['data']['nodes']; edges = flow['data']['edges']
ids = [n['id'] for n in nodes]
assert len(ids) == len(set(ids)), 'duplicate node ids'
node_ids = set(ids)
for e in edges:
    assert e['source'] in node_ids and e['target'] in node_ids, e['id']
print('file KB:', round(p.stat().st_size/1024))
print('nodes:', len(nodes), '| edges:', len(edges))
for n in nodes:
    if n['type'] != 'genericNode': continue
    tpl = n['data']['node'].get('template', {})
    extras = {k: tpl[k]['value'] for k in ('track','command') if k in tpl}
    print(' ', n['data']['node'].get('display_name','?').ljust(34), n['data']['type'].ljust(26), extras or '')

Created flows/LangGraph RAG Pipeline.json — a Langflow 1.10.2 flow, same export shape as Basic LLM Prompting.json, that rebuilds the LangGraph build_graph() node-for-node.

What's in it — 39 components + 3 notes, 66 edges:

One Custom Component per graph node, keeping the LangGraph names: user_input, the four cmd_* branches, generate_query_variants, retrieve, post_retrieval_filter, both validate_*_retrieval, both dedup_merge* + validators, the documents track (NAC → DC → LBC + validators), the learned-QA track (DC → LBC + validators), combine_tracks, generate_draft, check_answer_quality, generate_answer, auto_distillation, no_context_answer.
A single Data payload carries the whole GraphState node-to-node, using the key names from state.py.
Conditional edges from routes.py are components with several outputs that call self.stop() on the untaken branches — including both retry back-edges to generate_query_variants.
One shared Groq component fans its Language Model output into all 21 LLM nodes, so one API key configures everything. Prompts from prompts.py and thresholds from config.py are the real values, exposed as editable fields.
Verified: Graph.from_payload loads it in your installed Langflow (39 vertices, 66 edges, is_cyclic=True — 1.10.2 accepts the two back-edges natively, no Loop component needed). I also ran every component's Python end-to-end against a fake LLM and a real chromadb call: all 40 behaviour checks pass, covering routing, state threading, retries, and the empty-context path.

Two things to know before running it: point retrieve at your Chroma store (VECTOR_STORE_PATH, collections, all-MiniLM-L6-v2), and note that nodes.py wasn't in the attached context — the node bodies are reconstructions from graph.py/routes.py/state.py/config.py/prompts.py. The note in the top-left of the canvas lists exactly where that shows (token-overlap instead of embeddings in dedup_merge, NAC merging source-adjacent chunks, cmd_* reporting instead of touching MongoDB). Give me nodes.py and I'll replace those bodies with the real logic.

The generator lives in the scratchpad, not the repo — say the word if you'd like it checked in, or a docs/Status.md + README entry for the new flow.

import json
d=json.load(open('flows/Vector Store RAG.json',encoding='utf-8'))
for n in d['data']['nodes']:
    if n['data'].get('type') in ('Knowledge','MemoryBase'):
        nd=n['data']['node']
        print('=====', n['data']['type'], '| module:', nd.get('metadata',{}).get('module'))
        print('outputs:', json.dumps(nd['outputs'], indent=1)[:1200])
        for k,v in nd['template'].items():
            if k=='code': print('  code: <%d chars>'%len(v.get('value','')));continue

from urllib.parse import urlparse

import httpx
from langchain_community.embeddings.huggingface import HuggingFaceInferenceAPIEmbeddings

# Next update: use langchain_huggingface
from pydantic import SecretStr
from tenacity import retry, stop_after_attempt, wait_fixed

from lfx.base.embeddings.model import LCEmbeddingsModel
from lfx.field_typing import Embeddings
from lfx.io import MessageTextInput, Output, SecretStrInput
from lfx.utils.ssrf_httpx import ssrf_safe_httpx_get, validate_url_for_ssrf_or_raise


class HuggingFaceInferenceAPIEmbeddingsComponent(LCEmbeddingsModel):
    display_name = "Hugging Face Embeddings Inference"
    description = "Generate embeddings using Hugging Face Text Embeddings Inference (TEI)"
    documentation = "https://huggingface.co/docs/text-embeddings-inference/index"
    icon = "HuggingFace"
    name = "HuggingFaceInferenceAPIEmbeddings"

    inputs = [
        SecretStrInput(
            name="api_key",
            display_name="HuggingFace API Key",
            advanced=False,
            info="Required for non-local inference endpoints. Local inference does not require an API Key.",
        ),
        MessageTextInput(
            name="inference_endpoint",
            display_name="Inference Endpoint",
            required=True,
            value="https://api-inference.huggingface.co/models/",
            info="Custom inference endpoint URL.",
        ),
        MessageTextInput(
            name="model_name",
            display_name="Model Name",
            value="BAAI/bge-large-en-v1.5",
            info="The name of the model to use for text embeddings.",
            required=True,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="Embeddings", name="embeddings", method="build_embeddings"),
    ]

    def validate_inference_endpoint(self, inference_endpoint: str) -> bool:
        parsed_url = urlparse(inference_endpoint)
        if not all([parsed_url.scheme, parsed_url.netloc]):
            msg = (
                f"Invalid inference endpoint format: '{self.inference_endpoint}'. "
                "Please ensure the URL includes both a scheme (e.g., 'http://' or 'https://') and a domain name. "
                "Example: 'http://localhost:8080' or 'https://api.example.com'"
            )
            raise ValueError(msg)

        try:
            response = ssrf_safe_httpx_get(f"{inference_endpoint}/health", timeout=5)
        except httpx.HTTPError as e:
            msg = (
                f"Inference endpoint '{inference_endpoint}' is not responding. "
                "Please ensure the URL is correct and the service is running."
            )
            raise ValueError(msg) from e

        if response.status_code != httpx.codes.OK:
            msg = f"Hugging Face health check failed: {response.status_code}"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        # returning True to solve linting error
        return True

    def get_api_url(self) -> str:
        if "huggingface" in self.inference_endpoint.lower():
            return f"{self.inference_endpoint}"
        return self.inference_endpoint

    @retry(stop=stop_after_attempt(3), wait=wait_fixed(2))

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

from lfx.components._importing import import_mod

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from .huggingface import HuggingFaceEndpointsComponent
    from .huggingface_inference_api import HuggingFaceInferenceAPIEmbeddingsComponent

_dynamic_imports = {
    "HuggingFaceEndpointsComponent": "huggingface",
    "HuggingFaceInferenceAPIEmbeddingsComponent": "huggingface_inference_api",
}

__all__ = [
    "HuggingFaceEndpointsComponent",
    "HuggingFaceInferenceAPIEmbeddingsComponent",
]


def __getattr__(attr_name: str) -> Any:
    """Lazily import huggingface components on attribute access."""
    if attr_name not in _dynamic_imports:
        msg = f"module '{__name__}' has no attribute '{attr_name}'"
        raise AttributeError(msg)
    try:
        result = import_mod(attr_name, _dynamic_imports[attr_name], __spec__.parent)
    except (ModuleNotFoundError, ImportError, AttributeError) as e:
        msg = f"Could not import '{attr_name}' from '{__name__}': {e}"
        raise AttributeError(msg) from e
    globals()[attr_name] = result
    return result


def __dir__() -> list[str]:
    return list(__all__)

                    (doc, score) for doc, score in results if _chunk_matches_filter(doc.metadata, user_metadata_filter)
                ]
                results = results[: self.top_k]

            if self.include_embeddings and results:

                # ``_id`` when present and fall back to page content otherwise,
                # so KBs populated by direct file upload — whose chunks carry no
                # ``_id`` — still resolve. See ``_embedding_match_key``.
                wanted_keys = {_embedding_match_key(doc.page_content, doc.metadata) for doc, _score in results}
                async for batch in backend.iter_documents(include_embeddings=True):
                    for entry in batch:
                        if entry.embedding is None:
                            continue
                        key = _embedding_match_key(entry.content, entry.metadata)
                        if key in wanted_keys:
                            embeddings_by_key[key] = entry.embedding
                    if len(embeddings_by_key) == len(wanted_keys):
                        break

            data_list: list[Data] = []
            for doc, score in results:
                kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {"content": doc.page_content}
                if use_scores:
                    kwargs["_score"] = -1 * score
                if self.include_metadata:
                    kwargs.update(doc.metadata)
                if self.include_embeddings:
                    kwargs["_embeddings"] = embeddings_by_key.get(_embedding_match_key(doc.page_content, doc.metadata))
                data_list.append(Data(**kwargs))

            return DataFrame(data=data_list)
        finally:
            await backend.teardown()


def _embedding_match_key(content: str, metadata: dict[str, Any] | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """Build a stable key for aligning a retrieved chunk with its stored embedding.

    Embeddings are gathered separately (via ``iter_documents``) and then joined
    back onto the search results. Component-driven ingestion stamps a
    content-hash ``_id`` on every chunk, but direct file-upload ingestion
    (``KBIngestionHelper.perform_ingestion``) does not. Keying the join purely on
    ``_id`` therefore left every upload-populated KB with ``_embeddings: None``.

    We prefer ``_id`` when present (so legitimately distinct chunks that happen to
    share text aren't collapsed) and fall back to the chunk's page content
    otherwise. The content fallback is exact for the embedding use case: two
    chunks with identical text necessarily share the same embedding vector. The
    leading ``"id"`` / ``"content"`` tag namespaces the two key spaces so a mixed
    KB (some chunks with ``_id``, some without) never cross-matches.
    """
    doc_id = metadata.get("_id") if metadata else None
    if doc_id:
        return ("id", str(doc_id))
    return ("content", content or "")


def _parse_metadata_filter(raw: str | None) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    """Decode the ``metadata_filter`` input into a {key: [values]} map.

    Empty or malformed input maps to an empty filter so retrieval falls back
    to the unfiltered path. JSON errors are swallowed here rather than raised:
    surfacing component-config errors at the canvas node would break a flow
    run for what is meant to be an optional refinement.
    """
    if not raw:

93 lines
"""Give every component the LLM client its role calls for, plus a JSON-repair client.

Mirrors llm_setup.py: generation clients run warm (temperature 0.1 / 2048 tokens),
judge and JSON-repair clients run cold (temperature 0.0 / 1024 tokens).
"""

import re
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

OLD_LLM = (
    '        HandleInput(name="llm", display_name="Language Model", '
    'input_types=["LanguageModel"], required=True),\n'
)

GENERATION = '''        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="Groq client configured for generation (temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens).",
        ),
'''

JUDGE = '''        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Judge Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="Groq client configured for judging (temperature 0.0, 1024 output tokens).",
        ),
'''

REPAIR = '''        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
'''

# class name -> (role, parses_json)
ROLES = {
    "RagRetrievalValidator": ("judge", True),
    "RagMergeValidator": ("judge", True),
    "RagNacCompress": ("generation", True),
    "RagNacValidator": ("judge", True),
    "RagDcCompress": ("generation", True),
    "RagDcValidator": ("judge", True),
    "RagLbcCompress": ("generation", True),
    "RagLbcValidator": ("judge", True),
    "RagGenerateDraft": ("generation", False),
    "RagCheckAnswerQuality": ("judge", True),
    "RagGenerateAnswer": ("generation", False),
    "RagAutoDistillation": ("generation", True),
}

CLASS_RE = re.compile(r"^class (Rag\w+)\(Component\):", re.MULTILINE)


def patch(path: Path) -> None:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    # Split on the top-level "NAME = HEADER + r'''" assignments so each component
    # block can be rewritten independently.
    blocks = re.split(r"(?m)^(?=[A-Z_]+ = HEADER \+ r''')", text)
    out = []
    for block in blocks:
        match = CLASS_RE.search(block)
        name = match.group(1) if match else None
        if name in ROLES:
            role, parses_json = ROLES[name]
            replacement = GENERATION if role == "generation" else JUDGE
            if parses_json:
                replacement += REPAIR
            if OLD_LLM not in block:
                raise SystemExit(f"{name}: llm HandleInput one-liner not found")
            block = block.replace(OLD_LLM, replacement, 1)
            if parses_json:
                block = re.sub(
                    r"_parse_json\((.*?), (\{\}|\[\])\)",
                    r"_parse_json(\1, \2, repair_llm=self.repair_llm)",
                    block,
                )
        out.append(block)
    path.write_text("".join(out), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"patched {path.name}")


for filename in ("flow_components_a.py", "flow_components_b.py"):
    patch(HERE / filename)

cd "c:/Users/LOQ/Desktop/Projects/Langflow-learning-project" && .venv/Scripts/python.exe -c "
import json
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('flows/LangGraph RAG Pipeline.json')
flow = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
nodes = flow['data']['nodes']; edges = flow['data']['edges']
by_id = {n['id']: n for n in nodes}
assert len(by_id) == len(nodes)
for e in edges:
    assert e['source'] in by_id and e['target'] in by_id, e['id']
print('file KB:', round(p.stat().st_size/1024), '| nodes:', len(nodes), '| edges:', len(edges))
print()
print('--- built-in / library nodes ---')
for n in nodes:
    t = n['data'].get('type')
    if t in ('ChatInput','ChatOutput','GroqModel','Knowledge','MemoryBase','HuggingFaceInferenceAPIEmbeddings'):
        tpl = n['data']['node']['template']
        keys = [k for k in ('api_key','model_name','temperature','max_tokens','mode','top_k','knowledge_base','memory_base','filter_by_session','inference_endpoint') if k in tpl]
        cfg = {k: tpl[k].get('value') for k in keys}
        db = [k for k in keys if tpl[k].get('load_from_db')]
        print(' ', n['data']['node']['display_name'].ljust(36), t.ljust(34), cfg, ('load_from_db='+str(db)) if db else '')
print()
from collections import Counter
c = Counter((e['data']['sourceHandle']['id'], e['data']['targetHandle']['fieldName']) for e in edges)
print('--- model / embedding fan-out ---')
for (src, field), n in sorted(c.items()):
    if src.startswith(('GroqModel','HuggingFace','MemoryBase','Knowledge')):
        print('  {0} -> {1}: {2} edge(s)'.format(src.ljust(28), field.ljust(20), n))
"

flows/LangGraph RAG Pipeline.json is regenerated — 45 components, 98 edges. All five changes are in:

Groq API key — every Groq component has api_key set to GROQ_API_KEY with load_from_db: true, which is Langflow's global-variable mechanism. Create a global variable named GROQ_API_KEY in Settings → Global Variables and all three resolve it. (Same pattern for HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY on the embeddings node.)

Per-role LLM clients — one Groq node per role, mirroring llm_setup.py:

Client	Config	Wired to
Groq · Generation	temp 0.1, 2048 tokens	11 nodes: variants, merges, NAC/DC/LBC compression, draft, answer, distillation
Groq · Judge	temp 0.0, 1024 tokens	10 nodes: every validator + check_answer_quality
Groq · JSON Repair	temp 0.0, 1024 tokens	repair_llm on the 19 nodes that parse model JSON
The JSON Repair client is the fix_llm_output._LLM_Json_Repair path — it's only called when the cheap parse fails, and the node degrades to its default if repair also fails. All three keep llama-3.1-8b-instant; change the model dropdown per role if you want.

Knowledge nodes instead of Chroma — two Retrieve-mode Knowledge nodes (documents, learned_qa, top_k 4), copied from your Vector Store RAG export so the templates match. retrieve is now a pure adapter folding their rows into the graph state. One consequence worth knowing: Langflow fans out on edges, not payloads, so the Knowledge nodes run once per pass rather than once per variant. To keep the retry cycle meaningful, generate_query_variants gained a Search Query output that hands them the next unused variant on each retry — so retry 1 searches variant 1, retry 2 searches variant 2.

Memory Base instead of MongoDB — searched with the question, feeding user_input (parsed into prior_thumbdowns and blocked_variants) and all four cmd_* branches. It's read-only: Langflow captures the conversation itself, so there's no explicit thumbdown write-back like FeedbackStore.mark_last_bad. Noted on the canvas.

all-MiniLM-L6-v2 via HuggingFace — a Hugging Face Embeddings Inference node at sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2. Since Knowledge owns the retrieval embedding now, this feeds the similarity work EmbeddingManager did: near-duplicate removal in post_retrieval_filter (PRE_RETRIEVAL_SIM_THRESHOLD 0.95) and grouping in both dedup_merge nodes (MERGE_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD 0.90) — real cosine now, replacing the token-overlap approximation. Both fall back to token overlap if you disconnect it.

Verified: Graph.from_payload loads it (45 vertices, 98 edges, cycles intact with the Knowledge nodes correctly inside the retry loop), and 61 behaviour checks pass against fake Groq/HF/Knowledge/Memory clients. That testing caught one real bug — an unconnected optional handle resolves to "", not None, so removing the JSON Repair edge crashed the node; the checks are now duck-typed.

You'll still need to pick a knowledge base on each Knowledge node and a memory base on the Memory Base node after import — those are per-user resources that can't be baked into an export.
