"""Custom-component source for the retrieval half of the LangGraph RAG pipeline.

Node bodies follow app_workflow/nodes/*.py: user_input.py, query_variants.py,
retrieve.py, post_retrieve.py, validate_retrieval.py, dedup_merge.py.
"""

HEADER = r'''import json
import re

from lfx.custom.custom_component.component import Component
from lfx.io import (
    BoolInput,
    DataInput,
    DropdownInput,
    FloatInput,
    HandleInput,
    IntInput,
    MessageInput,
    MultilineInput,
    Output,
    StrInput,
)
from lfx.schema.data import Data
from lfx.schema.message import Message


def _state_of(payload):
    """Unwrap a Langflow Data payload (or list of them) into a GraphState dict.

    Inactive conditional branches emit an empty Data, so the first payload that
    actually carries state wins.
    """
    if payload is None:
        return {}
    items = payload if isinstance(payload, list) else [payload]
    for item in items:
        data = dict(getattr(item, "data", {}) or {})
        if data:
            return data
    return {}


def _switch(state, name, default=True):
    return bool((state.get("switches") or {}).get(name, default))


def _llm_text(llm, prompt):
    """Call the wired Language Model with a single prompt and return its text."""
    response = llm.invoke(prompt)
    content = getattr(response, "content", response)
    if isinstance(content, list):
        content = "".join(str(getattr(part, "text", part)) for part in content)
    return str(content or "").strip()


JSON_REPAIR_PROMPT = (
    "You are a JSON repair model. You receive a malformed or partially broken string and "
    "return a single, valid, complete JSON value.\n"
    "RULES:\n"
    "- Preserve all values from the raw input exactly - never fabricate data not present in it.\n"
    "- Each key must appear exactly once; if a key is duplicated, keep the last occurrence.\n"
    "- Output nothing outside the JSON value - no explanation, no comments, no code fences.\n\n"
    "RAW INPUT:\n"
)


def _parse_json_once(text):
    """Strip code fences, take the first balanced object/array. None when unrecoverable."""
    cleaned = re.sub(r"```[a-zA-Z]*", "", text or "").strip()
    start = next((i for i, ch in enumerate(cleaned) if ch in "[{"), -1)
    if start == -1:
        return None
    opener = cleaned[start]
    closer = "]" if opener == "[" else "}"
    depth = 0
    for index in range(start, len(cleaned)):
        if cleaned[index] == opener:
            depth += 1
        elif cleaned[index] == closer:
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0:
                try:
                    return json.loads(cleaned[start:index + 1])
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    return None
    return None


def _parse_json(text, default, repair_llm=None):
    """Parse LLM JSON, falling back to the JSON-repair model when the heuristics fail.

    Stands in for fix_llm_output(): the cheap tiers first, then the repair model. An
    optional handle left unconnected resolves to "" rather than None, so the repair
    client is duck-typed rather than compared against None.
    """
    parsed = _parse_json_once(text)
    if parsed is not None:
        return parsed
    if hasattr(repair_llm, "invoke") and (text or "").strip():
        parsed = _parse_json_once(_llm_text(repair_llm, JSON_REPAIR_PROMPT + str(text)[:4000]))
        if parsed is not None:
            return parsed
    return default


def _rows(table):
    """Normalise a Knowledge / Memory Base result into a list of plain row dicts."""
    if table is None:
        return []
    items = table if isinstance(table, list) else [table]
    rows = []
    for item in items:
        if item is None:
            continue
        if hasattr(item, "to_dict") and hasattr(item, "columns"):  # DataFrame
            rows.extend(item.to_dict(orient="records"))
            continue
        data = getattr(item, "data", None)
        if isinstance(data, dict):
            rows.append(dict(data))
        elif isinstance(item, dict):
            rows.append(dict(item))
    return rows


def _flatten_row(row, default_source):
    """Knowledge row -> the flat chunk shape retrieve._flatten_chunks produces.

    Downstream nodes read 'similarity_score' and a top-level int 'chunk_seq'
    (NAC's run detection depends on it), so the shape is preserved exactly.
    """
    chunk = {
        "content": row.get("content") or row.get("text") or "",
        "source": row.get("source") or row.get("file_name") or row.get("file_path") or default_source,
        "similarity_score": float(row.get("_score") or row.get("similarity_score") or 0.0),
    }
    sequence = row.get("chunk_seq", row.get("doc_index"))
    if isinstance(sequence, bool):
        sequence = None
    if isinstance(sequence, int):
        chunk["chunk_seq"] = sequence
    elif isinstance(sequence, str) and sequence.strip().lstrip("-").isdigit():
        chunk["chunk_seq"] = int(sequence)
    elif isinstance(sequence, float) and float(sequence).is_integer():
        chunk["chunk_seq"] = int(sequence)
    return chunk


def _cosine(left, right):
    dot = sum(a * b for a, b in zip(left, right))
    left_norm = sum(a * a for a in left) ** 0.5
    right_norm = sum(b * b for b in right) ** 0.5
    if not left_norm or not right_norm:
        return 0.0
    return dot / (left_norm * right_norm)


def _jaccard(left, right):
    left_tokens = set(re.findall(r"\w+", (left or "").lower()))
    right_tokens = set(re.findall(r"\w+", (right or "").lower()))
    if not left_tokens or not right_tokens:
        return 0.0
    return len(left_tokens & right_tokens) / float(len(left_tokens | right_tokens))


def _embed(embeddings, texts):
    """Embed with the wired embeddings model; None falls the caller back to token overlap."""
    if not hasattr(embeddings, "embed_documents") or not texts:
        return None
    try:
        return embeddings.embed_documents([text or "" for text in texts])
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - similarity is an optimisation, never fatal
        return None


def _similarity_matrix(embeddings, contents):
    """Return (similarity(i, j), mode) - embedding cosine when available."""
    vectors = _embed(embeddings, contents)
    if vectors and len(vectors) == len(contents):
        return lambda i, j: _cosine(vectors[i], vectors[j]), "embeddings"
    return lambda i, j: _jaccard(contents[i], contents[j]), "token-overlap"


def _build_context(compressed_docs):
    """Context format used by generate_draft / check_answer_quality / generate_answer."""
    return "\n\n".join(
        "[{0}] {1}".format(index + 1, doc.get("content", ""))
        for index, doc in enumerate(compressed_docs)
    )


def _chunks_block(chunks):
    """Indexed chunk block for the judge prompts."""
    return "\n\n".join(
        "[{0}] [Source: {1}]\n{2}".format(index, chunk.get("source", "?"), chunk.get("content", ""))
        for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks)
    )


def _field(track):
    """documents -> 'document', learned_qa -> 'learned_qa' (matches GraphState keys)."""
    return "document" if track == "documents" else "learned_qa"
'''


USER_INPUT = HEADER + r'''

class RagUserInput(Component):
    display_name = "user_input"
    description = (
        "Graph entry point (user_input_node). Detects the reserved commands "
        "(exit / quit / stats / learn / bad), loads the blocked variants and prior "
        "thumbdowns for this question, expands the blocked list with every "
        "reformulation those thumbdowns already tried, and emits the initial GraphState."
    )
    icon = "log-in"
    name = "RagUserInput"

    ROUTES = ("to_query", "to_stats", "to_learn", "to_bad", "to_exit")
    COMMANDS = {"exit": "exit", "quit": "exit", "stats": "stats", "learn": "learn", "bad": "bad"}

    inputs = [
        MessageInput(
            name="chat_message",
            display_name="Chat Message",
            info="The Playground message. A bare 'stats' / 'learn' / 'bad' / 'exit' is treated as a command.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="prior_memory",
            display_name="Memory Base Results",
            input_types=["DataFrame", "Table", "Data"],
            is_list=True,
            required=False,
            info=(
                "Long-term memory rows for this question, from the Memory Base node. Replaces "
                "feedback_store.load_failed_variants() + find_thumbdowns_for_query()."
            ),
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_sub_query_generation",
            display_name="ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION",
            value=True,
            info="Drives generate_query_variants and the retry budget in route_after_quality_check.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_compression_validation",
            display_name="ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION",
            value=True,
            info="Drives the NAC/DC validator routers on both compression tracks.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_answer_draft_creation",
            display_name="ENABLE_ANSWER_DRAFT_CREATION",
            value=True,
            info="False routes combine_tracks straight to generate_answer.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_answer_quality_check",
            display_name="ENABLE_ANSWER_QUALITY_CHECK",
            value=True,
            info="False routes generate_draft straight to generate_answer.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_auto_distillation",
            display_name="ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION",
            value=False,
            info="True routes generate_answer through auto_distillation.",
        ),
        IntInput(
            name="retry_limit",
            display_name="LLM_RESPONSE_RETRY_LIMIT",
            value=3,
            advanced=True,
            info="Maximum retrieval retries before the graph answers without context.",
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="Query State", name="to_query", method="route_query"),
        Output(display_name="cmd stats", name="to_stats", method="route_stats"),
        Output(display_name="cmd learn", name="to_learn", method="route_learn"),
        Output(display_name="cmd bad", name="to_bad", method="route_bad"),
        Output(display_name="cmd exit", name="to_exit", method="route_exit"),
    ]

    @staticmethod
    def _memory(rows):
        """Split Memory Base rows into thumbdowns, blocked variants and recalled turns."""
        thumbdowns = []
        blocked = []
        context = []
        for row in rows:
            content = str(row.get("content", "") or "")
            quality = str(row.get("quality", "") or "").upper()
            feedback = row.get("user_feedback") or row.get("feedback") or ""
            if quality == "USER_THUMBSDOWN" or feedback or "[thumbdown]" in content.lower():
                variants = row.get("variants")
                if isinstance(variants, str):
                    variants = [{"query": part.strip()} for part in variants.split("|") if part.strip()]
                thumbdowns.append(
                    {
                        "original_query": row.get("original_query", ""),
                        "bad_answer": row.get("bad_answer", content),
                        "user_feedback": str(feedback or content),
                        "variants": variants if isinstance(variants, list) else [],
                    }
                )
                continue
            failed = row.get("failed_variants")
            if isinstance(failed, str) and failed.strip():
                failed = [part.strip() for part in failed.split("|") if part.strip()]
            if isinstance(failed, list):
                blocked.extend(str(item) for item in failed if item)
            if content:
                context.append({"content": content, "similarity_score": float(row.get("_score") or 0.0)})

        # user_input.py: every reformulation recorded on a thumbdown is blocked too.
        existing = set(blocked)
        for thumbdown in thumbdowns:
            for variant in thumbdown.get("variants") or []:
                query = str((variant or {}).get("query", "")).strip()
                if query and query not in existing:
                    blocked.append(query)
                    existing.add(query)
        return thumbdowns, blocked, context

    def _initial_state(self):
        text = getattr(self.chat_message, "text", self.chat_message) or ""
        raw = str(text).strip()
        command = self.COMMANDS.get(raw.lower(), "")
        thumbdowns, blocked, memory_context = self._memory(_rows(self.prior_memory))
        return {
            "user_input": raw,
            "query": raw,
            "command": command,
            "user_feedback": "",
            "request_id": str(getattr(self, "_id", "")),
            "switches": {
                "ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION": self.enable_sub_query_generation,
                "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION": self.enable_compression_validation,
                "ENABLE_ANSWER_DRAFT_CREATION": self.enable_answer_draft_creation,
                "ENABLE_ANSWER_QUALITY_CHECK": self.enable_answer_quality_check,
                "ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION": self.enable_auto_distillation,
            },
            "retry_limit": self.retry_limit,
            "retry_count": 0,
            "query_variants": [],
            "blocked_variants": blocked,
            "prior_thumbdowns": thumbdowns,
            "prior_memory_chunks": memory_context,
            "retrieved_document_chunks": [],
            "retrieved_learned_qa_chunks": [],
            "variants_with_chunks": [],
            "newly_failed_variants": [],
            "compressed_docs": [],
            "answer": "",
        }

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._initial_state()
        route = {
            "stats": "to_stats",
            "learn": "to_learn",
            "bad": "to_bad",
            "exit": "to_exit",
        }.get(state.get("command", ""), "to_query")
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "route={0} query={1!r} blocked={2} thumbdowns={3}".format(
            route, state["query"], len(state["blocked_variants"]), len(state["prior_thumbdowns"])
        )
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_query(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_query")

    def route_stats(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_stats")

    def route_learn(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_learn")

    def route_bad(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_bad")

    def route_exit(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_exit")
'''


COMMAND = HEADER + r'''

class RagCommand(Component):
    display_name = "cmd"
    description = (
        "Terminal command branch (cmd_stats / cmd_learn / cmd_bad / cmd_exit). The MongoDB "
        "feedback store is replaced by the Memory Base node, so these report over the memory "
        "rows Langflow captured for this workflow."
    )
    icon = "terminal"
    name = "RagCommand"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="memory",
            display_name="Memory Base Results",
            input_types=["DataFrame", "Table", "Data"],
            is_list=True,
            required=False,
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="command",
            display_name="Command",
            options=["stats", "learn", "bad", "exit"],
            value="stats",
            info="Which cmd_* node of the LangGraph graph this instance stands for.",
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="Message", name="message", method="run_command")]

    NOTES = {
        "stats": "cmd_stats reports interaction counts and learned QA pairs; here it reports how "
                 "much long-term memory this workflow holds for the question.",
        "learn": "cmd_learn forces self_learner.run_distillation(); with a Memory Base the capture "
                 "happens every turn, so this reports what is already stored.",
        "bad": "cmd_bad marks the previous answer USER_THUMBSDOWN. The thumbdown text reaches the "
               "Memory Base through the normal capture rather than a MongoDB collection.",
        "exit": "cmd_exit ends the session (raises SystemExit in the LangGraph app).",
    }

    def run_command(self) -> Message:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        rows = _rows(self.memory)
        lines = [
            "[{0}] {1}".format(self.command, self.NOTES.get(self.command, "")),
            "",
            "Request           : {0!r}".format(state.get("user_input", "")),
            "Memory rows       : {0}".format(len(rows)),
            "Prior thumbdowns  : {0}".format(len(state.get("prior_thumbdowns") or [])),
            "Blocked variants  : {0}".format(len(state.get("blocked_variants") or [])),
        ]
        if self.command == "stats" and rows:
            sessions = {str(row.get("session_id", "")) for row in rows if row.get("session_id")}
            lines.append("Sessions recalled : {0}".format(len(sessions)))
        text = "\n".join(lines)
        self.status = text
        return Message(text=text)
'''


GENERATE_QUERY_VARIANTS = HEADER + r'''
ROLE_AND_RULES = __ROLE_AND_RULES__

PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS = __PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS__


class RagGenerateQueryVariants(Component):
    display_name = "generate_query_variants"
    description = (
        "query_variants.py. Builds the system prompt (role, retrieval budget, blocked "
        "variants, prior-failure history, process, priority feedback), asks the model for "
        "variants, strips blocked phrasings, then runs pre_retrieval_filter: inter-variant "
        "cosine dedup at PRE_RETRIEVAL_SIM_THRESHOLD followed by ranking against the user "
        "query and capping to the adaptive branch budget. retry_count increments on every "
        "pass, and each retry hands the Knowledge nodes the next variant."
    )
    icon = "git-branch"
    name = "RagGenerateQueryVariants"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm_tool in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="embeddings",
            display_name="Embeddings",
            input_types=["Embeddings"],
            required=False,
            info=(
                "all-MiniLM-L6-v2 - used by pre_retrieval_filter for inter-variant dedup and "
                "for ranking variants against the user query. Falls back to token overlap."
            ),
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="role_and_rules", display_name="Role and Rules", value=ROLE_AND_RULES),
        MultilineInput(
            name="process_instructions",
            display_name="Process Instructions",
            value=PROCESS_INSTRUCTIONS,
        ),
        FloatInput(
            name="pre_retrieval_sim_threshold",
            display_name="PRE_RETRIEVAL_SIM_THRESHOLD",
            value=0.95,
            advanced=True,
            info="Two variants this similar are the same query; the first one wins.",
        ),
        IntInput(name="budget_short", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_FOR_SHORT_QUESTIONS", value=2, advanced=True),
        IntInput(name="budget_medium", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_FOR_MEDIUM_QUESTIONS", value=2, advanced=True),
        IntInput(
            name="budget_conjunction",
            display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_FOR_CONJUNCTION_QUESTIONS",
            value=3,
            advanced=True,
        ),
        IntInput(name="short_word_count", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_SHORT_WORD_COUNT", value=4, advanced=True),
        IntInput(name="medium_word_count", display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_MEDIUM_WORD_COUNT", value=8, advanced=True),
        StrInput(
            name="conjunction_keywords",
            display_name="BRANCH_BUDGET_CONJUNCTION_KEYWORDS",
            value="and, or, both, versus, vs, compare, difference, how, why, explain, list, describe, contrast",
            advanced=True,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="build_variants"),
        Output(display_name="Search Query", name="search_query", method="build_search_query"),
    ]

    def _adaptive_branch_budget(self, user_query):
        """adaptive_branch_budget(): substring keyword test, exactly as in query_variants.py."""
        word_count = len(user_query.split())
        lowered = user_query.lower()
        keywords = [word.strip().lower() for word in (self.conjunction_keywords or "").split(",") if word.strip()]
        has_conjunction = any(keyword in lowered for keyword in keywords)
        if word_count <= self.short_word_count and not has_conjunction:
            return self.budget_short
        if word_count <= self.medium_word_count or not has_conjunction:
            return self.budget_medium
        return self.budget_conjunction

    def _build_system_prompt(self, blocked_variants, prior_thumbdowns, max_variants):
        """_build_system_prompt(): same blocks in the same order."""
        parts = [self.role_and_rules]
        parts.append(
            "RETRIEVAL BUDGET - Generate exactly {0} query variant(s) for this query."
            " Do NOT exceed this limit.".format(max_variants)
        )

        if blocked_variants:
            lines = "\n".join('  - "{0}"'.format(variant) for variant in blocked_variants)
            parts.append(
                "BLOCKED QUERY VARIANTS - These exact phrasings retrieved 0 useful chunks the"
                " last time this question was asked. Do NOT reuse them under any circumstances."
                " Generate different, semantically distinct reformulations:\n" + lines
            )

        if prior_thumbdowns:
            history = (
                "USER-FLAGGED PRIOR FAILURE - This same question (or a near-identical one) was"
                " asked before and the user explicitly marked the answer as bad. Below is what"
                " went wrong last time, including the exact query reformulations that were tried"
                " and the chunks they retrieved.\n"
                "  1. AVOID re-using the same query reformulations - they led to a bad answer.\n"
                "  2. AVOID grounding your answer in the same chunks unless you can clearly"
                " interpret them differently.\n"
                "  3. Try genuinely different angles, vocabulary, and sub-topics.\n"
            )
            for index, thumbdown in enumerate(prior_thumbdowns, start=1):
                history += "\n--- Prior failure #{0} ---\n".format(index)
                history += "Original query : {0}\n".format(thumbdown.get("original_query", ""))
                feedback = thumbdown.get("user_feedback", "")
                if feedback:
                    history += "User feedback  : {0}\n".format(feedback)
                bad_answer = thumbdown.get("bad_answer", "")
                if bad_answer:
                    snippet = bad_answer[:300] + ("..." if len(bad_answer) > 300 else "")
                    history += "Bad answer was : {0}\n".format(snippet)
                variants = thumbdown.get("variants") or []
                if variants:
                    history += "Reformulations that were tried (DO NOT REUSE):\n"
                    for variant in variants:
                        history += '  - "{0}"\n'.format(variant.get("query", ""))
                        document_chunks = variant.get("document_chunks", variant.get("chunks", [])) or []
                        learned_qa_chunks = variant.get("learned_qa_chunks", []) or []
                        for track_name, chunks in (
                            ("learned_qa", learned_qa_chunks),
                            ("documents", document_chunks),
                        ):
                            for chunk in chunks[:2]:
                                content = (chunk.get("content") or "").replace("\n", " ")
                                preview = content[:200] + ("..." if len(content) > 200 else "")
                                history += "      retrieved {0} [{1}]: {2}\n".format(
                                    track_name, chunk.get("source", "?"), preview
                                )
            parts.append(history)

        parts.append(self.process_instructions)

        if prior_thumbdowns:
            feedback_lines = [
                '  - "{0}"'.format((thumbdown.get("user_feedback") or "").strip())
                for thumbdown in prior_thumbdowns
                if (thumbdown.get("user_feedback") or "").strip()
            ]
            if feedback_lines:
                parts.append(
                    "PRIORITY - USER FEEDBACK MUST BE ADDRESSED\n"
                    "The user previously flagged an answer to this exact question as bad and told"
                    " you specifically what was missing or wrong:\n"
                    + "\n".join(feedback_lines)
                    + "\n\nYou MUST generate at least one retrieve_documents query that directly"
                    " targets the topics or facts described in this feedback. Do not treat this as"
                    " a passive hint - it is your primary retrieval objective."
                )

        return "\n\n".join(parts)

    def _pre_retrieval_filter(self, user_query, raw_variants, max_variants):
        """pre_retrieval_filter(): inter-variant dedup, then rank against the query and cap."""
        if not raw_variants:
            return [], [], "n/a"

        texts = [variant.strip().lower() for variant in raw_variants]
        vectors = _embed(self.embeddings, texts + [user_query.strip().lower()])
        mode = "embeddings" if vectors and len(vectors) == len(texts) + 1 else "token-overlap"

        def similarity(left_index, right_index):
            if mode == "embeddings":
                return _cosine(vectors[left_index], vectors[right_index])
            return _jaccard(texts[left_index], texts[right_index])

        query_index = len(texts)

        kept_indices = []
        redundant = []
        for index in range(len(texts)):
            if any(similarity(index, kept) >= self.pre_retrieval_sim_threshold for kept in kept_indices):
                redundant.append(raw_variants[index])
                continue
            kept_indices.append(index)

        if mode == "embeddings":
            scores = {index: _cosine(vectors[index], vectors[query_index]) for index in kept_indices}
        else:
            scores = {index: _jaccard(texts[index], user_query.strip().lower()) for index in kept_indices}
        ordered = sorted(kept_indices, key=lambda index: scores[index], reverse=True)

        if max_variants is not None and len(ordered) > max_variants:
            redundant.extend(raw_variants[index] for index in ordered[max_variants:])
            ordered = ordered[:max_variants]

        return [raw_variants[index] for index in ordered], redundant, mode

    def build_variants(self) -> Data:
        return Data(data=self._resolve())

    def build_search_query(self) -> Message:
        """The query handed to the Knowledge nodes for this pass.

        Langflow fans out on edges, not on payloads, so the Knowledge nodes run once per
        pass instead of once per variant. retry_count therefore selects which variant is
        searched, which keeps the retry cycle meaningful.
        """
        state = self._resolve()
        variants = state.get("query_variants") or [state.get("user_input", "")]
        index = min(max(0, int(state.get("retry_count", 1)) - 1), len(variants) - 1)
        chosen = variants[index] if variants else ""
        self.status = "search query (pass {0}): {1!r}".format(state.get("retry_count", 1), chosen)
        return Message(text=chosen)

    def _resolve(self):
        cached = getattr(self, "_resolved_state", None)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        state = self._build()
        self._resolved_state = state
        return state

    def _build(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        user_query = state.get("user_input") or state.get("query") or ""

        # query_variants.py increments retry_count on every pass, disabled path included.
        state["retry_count"] = int(state.get("retry_count", 0)) + 1

        if state["retry_count"] > 1:
            # A retry re-runs retrieval from scratch and blocks what has already been tried.
            state["blocked_variants"] = list(
                dict.fromkeys(list(state.get("blocked_variants") or []) + list(state.get("query_variants") or []))
            )
            for key in (
                "retrieved_document_chunks",
                "retrieved_learned_qa_chunks",
                "variants_with_chunks",
                "newly_failed_variants",
            ):
                state[key] = []

        if not _switch(state, "ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION"):
            state["query_variants"] = [user_query]
            self.status = "sub-query generation disabled -> [{0!r}]".format(user_query)
            return state

        max_variants = self._adaptive_branch_budget(user_query)
        state["branch_budget"] = max_variants
        blocked_variants = list(state.get("blocked_variants") or [])
        prior_thumbdowns = list(state.get("prior_thumbdowns") or [])

        prompt = "{0}\n\nUSER QUESTION:\n{1}".format(
            self._build_system_prompt(blocked_variants, prior_thumbdowns, max_variants), user_query
        )
        parsed = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), [], repair_llm=self.repair_llm)

        raw_variants = []
        seen = set()
        for item in parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else []:
            candidate = str(item.get("query", "") if isinstance(item, dict) else item).strip()
            if candidate and candidate.lower() not in seen:
                raw_variants.append(candidate)
                seen.add(candidate.lower())

        if not raw_variants:
            raw_variants = [user_query]

        blocked_set = {variant.strip().lower() for variant in blocked_variants}
        non_failed = [variant for variant in raw_variants if variant.strip().lower() not in blocked_set]
        if not non_failed:
            non_failed = raw_variants

        query_variants, redundant, mode = self._pre_retrieval_filter(user_query, non_failed, max_variants)
        if not query_variants:
            query_variants = non_failed[:max_variants] or [user_query]

        state["query_variants"] = query_variants
        self.status = "budget={0} pass={1} kept={2} redundant={3} ({4})\n{5}".format(
            max_variants, state["retry_count"], len(query_variants), len(redundant), mode,
            json.dumps(query_variants, indent=2),
        )
        return state
'''


RETRIEVE = HEADER + r'''

class RagRetrieve(Component):
    display_name = "retrieve"
    description = (
        "retrieve.py against Langflow's Knowledge nodes instead of the Chroma retriever. "
        "Applies the per-track score thresholds the retriever applies, flattens each row to "
        "{content, source, similarity_score, chunk_seq} and records the per-variant entry "
        "that post_retrieval_filter and the feedback path consume."
    )
    icon = "search"
    name = "RagRetrieve"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="documents_results",
            display_name="Documents Knowledge",
            input_types=["DataFrame", "Table", "Data"],
            is_list=True,
            required=False,
            info="Retrieve-mode output of the Knowledge node holding the ingested documents.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="learned_qa_results",
            display_name="Learned-QA Knowledge",
            input_types=["DataFrame", "Table", "Data"],
            is_list=True,
            required=False,
            info="Retrieve-mode output of the Knowledge node holding the distilled QA pairs.",
        ),
        IntInput(name="top_k", display_name="RETRIEVAL_TOP_K", value=4, advanced=True),
        IntInput(name="top_l", display_name="RETRIEVAL_TOP_L", value=4, advanced=True),
        FloatInput(
            name="documents_min_similarity",
            display_name="DOCUMENTS_MIN_SIMILARITY",
            value=0.53,
            info="doc_score_threshold passed to retriever.retrieve_separate().",
        ),
        FloatInput(
            name="learned_qa_min_similarity",
            display_name="LEARNED_QA_MIN_SIMILARITY",
            value=0.57,
            info="learned_score_threshold passed to retriever.retrieve_separate().",
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="retrieve")]

    @staticmethod
    def _track(rows, default_source, floor, limit):
        chunks = [_flatten_row(row, default_source) for row in rows]
        chunks = [chunk for chunk in chunks if chunk["content"].strip()]
        chunks = [chunk for chunk in chunks if chunk["similarity_score"] >= floor]
        chunks.sort(key=lambda chunk: chunk["similarity_score"], reverse=True)
        return chunks[:limit]

    def retrieve(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        variants = list(state.get("query_variants") or [])
        if not variants and state.get("user_input"):
            variants = [state["user_input"]]
        index = min(max(0, int(state.get("retry_count", 1)) - 1), max(0, len(variants) - 1))
        query = variants[index] if variants else state.get("user_input", "")

        documents = self._track(
            _rows(self.documents_results), "knowledge", self.documents_min_similarity, self.top_k
        )
        learned = self._track(
            _rows(self.learned_qa_results), "learned_qa", self.learned_qa_min_similarity, self.top_l
        )

        variant_entry = {
            "query": query,
            "document_chunks": [
                {"content": chunk["content"], "source": chunk["source"]} for chunk in documents
            ],
            "learned_qa_chunks": [
                {"content": chunk["content"], "source": chunk["source"]} for chunk in learned
            ],
        }

        state["query"] = query
        state["retrieved_document_chunks"] = documents
        state["retrieved_learned_qa_chunks"] = learned
        state["variants_with_chunks"] = list(state.get("variants_with_chunks") or []) + [variant_entry]
        state["newly_failed_variants"] = [] if (documents or learned) else [query]
        self.status = "query={0!r} docs={1} learned_qa={2}".format(query, len(documents), len(learned))
        return Data(data=state)
'''


POST_RETRIEVAL_FILTER = HEADER + r'''

class RagPostRetrievalFilter(Component):
    display_name = "post_retrieval_filter"
    description = (
        "post_retrieve.py. Fan-in barrier after retrieval: discards variant contributions "
        "whose combined (content, source) set is identical to a variant already kept, then "
        "filters the accumulated retrieved_* lists down to the chunks that belong to the "
        "kept variants. Chunk data (similarity_score, chunk_seq) is preserved by filtering "
        "rather than rebuilding."
    )
    icon = "filter"
    name = "RagPostRetrievalFilter"

    inputs = [DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True)]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="filter_chunks")]

    def filter_chunks(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        variants = state.get("variants_with_chunks") or []
        all_document_chunks = state.get("retrieved_document_chunks") or []
        all_learned_chunks = state.get("retrieved_learned_qa_chunks") or []

        seen_sets = set()
        kept_keys = set()
        redundant = []

        for entry in variants:
            document_chunks = entry.get("document_chunks") or []
            learned_chunks = entry.get("learned_qa_chunks") or []
            combined = frozenset(
                (chunk.get("content", ""), chunk.get("source", ""))
                for chunk in (document_chunks + learned_chunks)
            )
            if not combined:
                # variant retrieved nothing - skip, don't count as redundant
                continue
            if combined in seen_sets:
                redundant.append(entry.get("query", ""))
            else:
                seen_sets.add(combined)
                kept_keys.update(combined)

        if not redundant:
            filtered_documents = list(all_document_chunks)
            filtered_learned = list(all_learned_chunks)
        else:
            filtered_documents = [
                chunk for chunk in all_document_chunks
                if (chunk.get("content", ""), chunk.get("source", "")) in kept_keys
            ]
            filtered_learned = [
                chunk for chunk in all_learned_chunks
                if (chunk.get("content", ""), chunk.get("source", "")) in kept_keys
            ]

        state["post_filtered_document_chunks"] = filtered_documents
        state["post_filtered_learned_qa_chunks"] = filtered_learned
        self.status = "redundant_variants={0}  doc={1}->{2}  qa={3}->{4}".format(
            len(redundant), len(all_document_chunks), len(filtered_documents),
            len(all_learned_chunks), len(filtered_learned),
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


RETRIEVAL_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT = __RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagRetrievalValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_retrieval"
    description = (
        "validate_retrieval.py. Reads post_filtered_* (falling back to retrieved_*) and drops "
        "only the chunks the judge explicitly marks relevant=false - anything the judge omits "
        "is kept, and an UNKNOWN verdict keeps everything. Pass-through when "
        "ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_VALIDATION is off."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagRetrievalValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Judge Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="judge_llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.0, 1024 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_retrieval_validation",
            display_name="ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_VALIDATION",
            value=True,
        ),
        MultilineInput(
            name="judge_prompt",
            display_name="Retrieval Judge Prompt",
            value=RETRIEVAL_JUDGE_PROMPT,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = list(
            state.get("post_filtered_{0}_chunks".format(field))
            or state.get("retrieved_{0}_chunks".format(field))
            or []
        )
        query = state.get("query") or state.get("user_input") or ""

        if not chunks:
            state["validated_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = []
            self.status = "no chunks - skipping"
            return Data(data=state)

        if not self.enable_retrieval_validation:
            state["validated_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
            self.status = "disabled - passing through {0} chunk(s)".format(len(chunks))
            return Data(data=state)

        prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(query=query, chunks_block=_chunks_block(chunks))
        result = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {}, repair_llm=self.repair_llm)
        verdict = str((result or {}).get("verdict", "UNKNOWN")).upper() if isinstance(result, dict) else "UNKNOWN"

        irrelevant = set()
        if verdict != "UNKNOWN" and isinstance(result, dict):
            for item in result.get("per_chunk") or []:
                if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("relevant") is False and isinstance(item.get("index"), int):
                    irrelevant.add(item["index"])

        kept = [chunk for index, chunk in enumerate(chunks) if index not in irrelevant]
        state["validated_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = kept
        state["{0}_retrieval_verdict".format(field)] = verdict
        self.status = "verdict={0} | kept {1}/{2}".format(verdict, len(kept), len(chunks))
        return Data(data=state)
'''


DEDUP_MERGE = HEADER + r'''
CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT = __CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT__


class RagDedupMerge(Component):
    display_name = "dedup_merge"
    description = (
        "dedup_merge.py. Embeds every chunk in the track, groups pairs at or above "
        "MERGE_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD, and consolidates each group with the same merge prompt "
        "NAC uses. The group's first member is replaced by the merged chunk and the rest are "
        "dropped; the surviving chunks are cleaned back to {content, source, similarity_score, "
        "chunk_seq}."
    )
    icon = "combine"
    name = "RagDedupMerge"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="embeddings",
            display_name="Embeddings",
            input_types=["Embeddings"],
            required=False,
            info="all-MiniLM-L6-v2, matching embedding_manager; falls back to token overlap.",
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(name="enable_dedup_merge", display_name="ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_DEDUP_MERGE", value=True),
        FloatInput(
            name="merge_similarity_threshold",
            display_name="MERGE_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD",
            value=0.9,
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="merge_prompt", display_name="Chunk Merge Prompt", value=CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="merge")]

    @staticmethod
    def _clean(chunk):
        cleaned = {"content": chunk.get("content", ""), "source": chunk.get("source", "?")}
        if "similarity_score" in chunk:
            cleaned["similarity_score"] = chunk["similarity_score"]
        if isinstance(chunk.get("chunk_seq"), int):
            cleaned["chunk_seq"] = chunk["chunk_seq"]
        return cleaned

    def _merge_group(self, members):
        """_merge_similar_chunks(): consolidate, keeping chunk_seq when the source is single."""
        formatted = "\n\n".join(
            "[Source: {0}]\n{1}".format(json.dumps(chunk.get("source", "?")), json.dumps(chunk.get("content", "")))
            for chunk in members
        )
        result = _parse_json(
            _llm_text(self.llm, self.merge_prompt.format(chunks_block=formatted)),
            {},
            repair_llm=self.repair_llm,
        )
        content = (result or {}).get("content") if isinstance(result, dict) else ""
        if not content:
            # merge failed - keep the first chunk only, as _merge_similar_chunks does
            return None
        sources = result.get("sources") or [chunk.get("source", "?") for chunk in members]
        merged = {"content": content, "source": ", ".join(dict.fromkeys(str(item) for item in sources))}
        sequences = [chunk.get("chunk_seq") for chunk in members if isinstance(chunk.get("chunk_seq"), int)]
        if sequences and len({chunk.get("source", "") for chunk in members}) == 1:
            merged["chunk_seq"] = min(sequences)
        return merged

    def merge(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        input_chunks = [
            chunk for chunk in (state.get("validated_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])
            if (chunk.get("content") or "").strip()
        ]

        if not input_chunks:
            state["dedup_merged_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = []
            state["dedup_merge_{0}_pairs".format(field)] = []
            self.status = "no chunks - skipping"
            return Data(data=state)

        if not self.enable_dedup_merge:
            state["dedup_merged_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = list(input_chunks)
            state["dedup_merge_{0}_pairs".format(field)] = []
            self.status = "disabled - passing through {0} chunk(s)".format(len(input_chunks))
            return Data(data=state)

        contents = [chunk.get("content", "") for chunk in input_chunks]
        similarity, mode = _similarity_matrix(self.embeddings, contents)

        claimed = set()
        merge_groups = []
        for index in range(len(input_chunks)):
            if index in claimed:
                continue
            group = [index]
            for other in range(index + 1, len(input_chunks)):
                if other in claimed:
                    continue
                if similarity(index, other) >= self.merge_similarity_threshold:
                    group.append(other)
                    claimed.add(other)
            if len(group) > 1:
                claimed.add(index)
                merge_groups.append(group)

        working = list(input_chunks)
        merge_pairs = []
        indices_to_drop = set()
        for group in merge_groups:
            keeper = group[0]
            source_chunks = [input_chunks[index] for index in group]
            merged = self._merge_group(source_chunks)
            if merged is None:
                continue
            working[keeper] = merged
            merge_pairs.append({"source_chunks": source_chunks, "merged": merged})
            indices_to_drop.update(group[1:])

        result_chunks = [
            self._clean(chunk) for index, chunk in enumerate(working) if index not in indices_to_drop
        ]

        state["dedup_merged_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = result_chunks
        state["dedup_merge_{0}_pairs".format(field)] = merge_pairs
        self.status = "{0} -> {1} chunk(s), {2} merge group(s) (similarity: {3})".format(
            len(input_chunks), len(result_chunks), len(merge_pairs), mode
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


MERGE_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT = __MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagMergeValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_dedup_merge"
    description = (
        "validate_dedup_merge_* in dedup_merge.py. Runs the faithfulness judge over every "
        "merge this track performed and records each verdict. Like the LangGraph node it is "
        "observational - the chunks are not modified - and it is a no-op when "
        "ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_DEDUP_MERGE_VALIDATION is off."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagMergeValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Judge Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="judge_llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.0, 1024 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_dedup_merge_validation",
            display_name="ENABLE_RETRIEVAL_DEDUP_MERGE_VALIDATION",
            value=True,
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="judge_prompt", display_name="Merge Judge Prompt", value=MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        pairs = list(state.get("dedup_merge_{0}_pairs".format(field)) or [])

        if not self.enable_dedup_merge_validation:
            self.status = "disabled"
            return Data(data=state)
        if not pairs:
            self.status = "no merge pairs to validate"
            return Data(data=state)

        verdicts = []
        for index, pair in enumerate(pairs):
            prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
                sources_block=_chunks_block(pair.get("source_chunks") or []),
                merged_block=(pair.get("merged") or {}).get("content", ""),
            )
            result = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {}, repair_llm=self.repair_llm)
            fabricated = (result or {}).get("fabricated_claims") or [] if isinstance(result, dict) else []
            dropped = (result or {}).get("dropped_claims") or [] if isinstance(result, dict) else []
            verdicts.append(
                {
                    "pair": index,
                    "verdict": "FAIL" if (fabricated or dropped) else "PASS",
                    "fabricated_claims": fabricated,
                    "dropped_claims": dropped,
                }
            )

        state["dedup_merge_{0}_verdicts".format(field)] = verdicts
        self.status = "\n".join(
            "pair {0}: verdict={1}  fabricated={2}  dropped={3}".format(
                entry["pair"], entry["verdict"], len(entry["fabricated_claims"]), len(entry["dropped_claims"])
            )
            for entry in verdicts
        )
        return Data(data=state)

NAC = HEADER + r'''
CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT = __CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT__


class RagNacCompress(Component):
    display_name = "NAC_documents"
    description = (
        "nac.py - Neighbor-Aware Compression. Chunks carrying an integer chunk_seq are sorted "
        "by (source, chunk_seq) and split into runs of consecutive sequence numbers from the "
        "same source; every run longer than one is consolidated by the merge prompt and gets "
        "chunk_seq_start / chunk_seq_end instead of chunk_seq. Chunks without a chunk_seq pass "
        "through untouched and are appended at the end."
    )
    icon = "layers"
    name = "RagNacCompress"

    ROUTES = ("to_validator", "to_dc")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        BoolInput(name="enable_nac", display_name="ENABLE_NAC_COMPRESSION", value=True),
        MultilineInput(name="merge_prompt", display_name="Chunk Merge Prompt", value=CHUNK_MERGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Validator", name="to_validator", method="route_validator"),
        Output(display_name="To DC", name="to_dc", method="route_dc"),
    ]

    def _merge_run(self, members):
        formatted = "\n\n".join(
            "[Source: {0}]\n{1}".format(json.dumps(chunk.get("source", "?")), json.dumps(chunk.get("content", "")))
            for chunk in members
        )
        result = _parse_json(
            _llm_text(self.llm, self.merge_prompt.format(chunks_block=formatted)),
            {},
            repair_llm=self.repair_llm,
        )
        content = (result or {}).get("content") if isinstance(result, dict) else ""
        if not content:
            return None
        sources = result.get("sources") or [chunk.get("source", "?") for chunk in members]
        return {"content": content, "source": ", ".join(dict.fromkeys(str(item) for item in sources))}

    def _run(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        accumulated = list(state.get("dedup_merged_document_chunks") or [])

        if not accumulated:
            state["nac_output_document_chunks"] = []
            state["nac_merge_pairs_documents"] = []
            return state, "no chunks"

        if not self.enable_nac:
            state["nac_output_document_chunks"] = accumulated
            state["nac_merge_pairs_documents"] = []
            return state, "disabled - passing through {0} chunk(s)".format(len(accumulated))

        eligible = [chunk for chunk in accumulated if isinstance(chunk.get("chunk_seq"), int)]
        ineligible = [chunk for chunk in accumulated if not isinstance(chunk.get("chunk_seq"), int)]

        if not eligible:
            state["nac_output_document_chunks"] = accumulated
            state["nac_merge_pairs_documents"] = []
            return state, "no chunks carry chunk_seq - skipping compression"

        eligible.sort(key=lambda chunk: (chunk.get("source", ""), chunk["chunk_seq"]))
        runs = []
        current = [eligible[0]]
        for chunk in eligible[1:]:
            previous = current[-1]
            same_source = chunk.get("source") == previous.get("source")
            consecutive = chunk["chunk_seq"] == previous["chunk_seq"] + 1
            if same_source and consecutive:
                current.append(chunk)
            else:
                runs.append(current)
                current = [chunk]
        runs.append(current)

        result_chunks = []
        merge_pairs = []
        for run in runs:
            if len(run) == 1:
                result_chunks.append(run[0])
                continue
            candidate = self._merge_run(run)
            if candidate is None:
                # _merge_similar_chunks falls back to the first chunk of the run
                result_chunks.append(run[0])
                continue
            candidate["chunk_seq_start"] = run[0]["chunk_seq"]
            candidate["chunk_seq_end"] = run[-1]["chunk_seq"]
            candidate.pop("chunk_seq", None)
            result_chunks.append(candidate)
            merge_pairs.append({"source_chunks": run, "merged": candidate})

        result_chunks.extend(ineligible)

        state["nac_output_document_chunks"] = result_chunks
        state["nac_merge_pairs_documents"] = merge_pairs
        return state, "{0} -> {1} chunk(s), {2} neighbour run(s) merged".format(
            len(accumulated), len(result_chunks), len(merge_pairs)
        )

    def _emit(self, target):
        state, status = self._run()
        route = "to_validator" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION") else "to_dc"
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "{0} | route={1}".format(status, route)
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_validator(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_validator")

    def route_dc(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_dc")
'''

MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT = __MERGE_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagNacValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_NAC_documents"
    description = (

    icon = "shield-check"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Judge Model",
