DC = HEADER + r'''
DC_SCAN_PROMPT = __DC_SCAN_PROMPT__

_SOURCE_TAG_RE = re.compile(r"\[Source:[^\]]*\]", re.IGNORECASE)


class RagDcCompress(Component):
    display_name = "DC_documents"
    description = (
        "dc.py. Slides a DC_WINDOW_SIZE window over the track and asks the model which "
        "sentences from DIFFERENT chunks state the same fact. Groups must span at least two "
        "distinct chunks; the lowest chunk index in a group keeps the sentence and the others "
        "lose it. A removal that changes nothing, or that would leave under 50 characters, is "
        "skipped."
    )
    icon = "scissors"
    name = "RagDcCompress"

    ROUTES = ("to_validator", "to_lbc")
    MIN_CLEANED_CHARS = 50

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(name="enable_dc", display_name="ENABLE_DC_COMPRESSION", value=True),
        IntInput(name="window_size", display_name="DC_WINDOW_SIZE", value=3),
        MultilineInput(name="scan_prompt", display_name="DC Scan Prompt", value=DC_SCAN_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Validator", name="to_validator", method="route_validator"),
        Output(display_name="To LBC", name="to_lbc", method="route_lbc"),
    ]

    def _input_chunks(self, state):
        if self.track == "documents":
            return list(state.get("nac_output_document_chunks") or [])
        return list(state.get("dedup_merged_learned_qa_chunks") or [])

    @staticmethod
    def _window_block(window):
        blocks = []
        for local_index, chunk in enumerate(window):
            source = chunk.get("source", "?")
            content = _SOURCE_TAG_RE.sub("", (chunk.get("content") or "")).strip()
            blocks.append("--- chunk {0} (source: {1}) ---\n{2}".format(local_index, source, content))
        return "\n\n".join(blocks)

    def _run(self):
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = self._input_chunks(state)

        if not self.enable_dc:
            state["dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
            state["dc_groups_per_window_{0}".format(self.track)] = []
            return state, "disabled - passing through {0} chunk(s)".format(len(chunks))

        if len(chunks) < 2:
            state["dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = list(chunks)
            state["dc_groups_per_window_{0}".format(self.track)] = []
            return state, "fewer than 2 chunks - nothing to scan"

        result = [dict(chunk) for chunk in chunks]
        window_size = max(2, self.window_size)
        groups_per_window = []
        total_removed = 0

        for window_start in range(0, len(result), window_size):
            window = result[window_start:min(window_start + window_size, len(result))]
            if len(window) < 2:
                continue

            prompt = self.scan_prompt.format(chunks_block=self._window_block(window))
            flagged = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), [], repair_llm=self.repair_llm)
            if not isinstance(flagged, list) or not flagged:
                continue

            valid_groups = []
            for group in flagged:
                members = (group or {}).get("members") if isinstance(group, dict) else None
                if not isinstance(members, list) or len(members) < 2:
                    continue
                clean_members = []
                for member in members:
                    if not isinstance(member, dict):
                        continue
                    local_index = member.get("chunk_index")
                    sentence = (member.get("sentence") or "").strip()
                    if not isinstance(local_index, int) or isinstance(local_index, bool) or not sentence:
                        continue
                    if local_index < 0 or local_index >= len(window):
                        continue
                    clean_members.append({"chunk_index": local_index, "sentence": sentence})
                if len(clean_members) < 2:
                    continue
                # A group must span at least two chunks - intra-chunk repetition is not
                # cross-chunk redundancy.
                if len({member["chunk_index"] for member in clean_members}) < 2:
                    continue
                valid_groups.append(clean_members)

            if not valid_groups:
                continue

            groups_per_window.append({"window_chunks": list(window), "groups": valid_groups})

            # The lowest chunk index keeps the sentence; every other member loses it.
            removals = {}
            for members in valid_groups:
                keeper = min(member["chunk_index"] for member in members)
                for member in members:
                    if member["chunk_index"] != keeper:
                        removals.setdefault(member["chunk_index"], []).append(member["sentence"])

            for local_index, sentences in removals.items():
                global_index = window_start + local_index
                original_chunk = result[global_index]
                original_content = (original_chunk.get("content") or "").strip()

                cleaned = original_content
                for sentence in sentences:
                    if sentence in cleaned:
                        cleaned = cleaned.replace(sentence, "").strip()
                    else:
                        normalised_sentence = " ".join(sentence.split())
                        normalised_content = " ".join(cleaned.split())
                        if normalised_sentence in normalised_content:
                            cleaned = normalised_content.replace(normalised_sentence, "").strip()

                if cleaned == original_content:
                    continue
                if len(cleaned.strip()) < self.MIN_CLEANED_CHARS:
                    continue

                result[global_index] = {**original_chunk, "content": cleaned}
                total_removed += len(sentences)

        state["dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = result
        state["dc_groups_per_window_{0}".format(self.track)] = groups_per_window
        return state, "{0} redundant passage(s) removed across {1} chunk(s)".format(total_removed, len(result))

    def _emit(self, target):
        state, status = self._run()
        route = "to_validator" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION") else "to_lbc"
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "{0} | route={1}".format(status, route)
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_validator(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_validator")

    def route_lbc(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_lbc")
'''


DC_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
REDUNDANCY_JUDGE_PROMPT = __REDUNDANCY_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagDcValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_DC_documents"
    description = (
        "validate_dc_* in dc.py. Judges each window's redundancy groups and records how many "
        "were confirmed and rejected. Observational, like the LangGraph node - the chunks DC "
        "produced are not modified."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagDcValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Judge Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="judge_llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.0, 1024 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        MultilineInput(
            name="judge_prompt",
            display_name="Redundancy Judge Prompt",
            value=REDUNDANCY_JUDGE_PROMPT,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        windows = list(state.get("dc_groups_per_window_{0}".format(self.track)) or [])
        if not windows:
            self.status = "no redundancy groups to validate"
            return Data(data=state)

        summaries = []
        for index, entry in enumerate(windows):
            groups = entry.get("groups") or []
            prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
                chunks_block=_chunks_block(entry.get("window_chunks") or []),
                groups_block=json.dumps([{"members": members} for members in groups], indent=2),
            )
            verdicts = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), [], repair_llm=self.repair_llm)
            confirmed = sum(
                1 for item in verdicts
                if isinstance(item, dict) and str(item.get("verdict", "")).upper() == "CONFIRMED"
            )
            rejected = sum(
                1 for item in verdicts
                if isinstance(item, dict) and str(item.get("verdict", "")).upper() == "REJECTED"
            )
            summaries.append({"window": index, "confirmed": confirmed, "rejected": rejected})

        state["dc_validation_{0}".format(self.track)] = summaries
        self.status = "\n".join(
            "window {0}: confirmed={1}  rejected={2}".format(
                entry["window"], entry["confirmed"], entry["rejected"]
            )
            for entry in summaries
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


LBC = HEADER + r'''
LBC_COMPRESS_PROMPT = __LBC_COMPRESS_PROMPT__

LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS = __LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS__


class RagLbcCompress(Component):
    display_name = "LBC_documents"
    description = (
        "lbc.py - query-focused compression, one call per chunk. __IRRELEVANT__ drops the "
        "chunk; three guards keep the original instead: retention below "
        "LBC_MIN_RETENTION_RATIO (over-compression), output longer than the input "
        "(over-expansion), and output identical to the input (no change). Only genuinely "
        "compressed chunks become validation pairs."
    )
    icon = "minimize-2"
    name = "RagLbcCompress"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        BoolInput(name="enable_lbc", display_name="ENABLE_LBC_COMPRESSION", value=True),
        FloatInput(
            name="min_retention_ratio",
            display_name="LBC_MIN_RETENTION_RATIO",
            value=0.35,
            info="A compressed chunk retaining less than this fraction is rejected.",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="compress_prompt", display_name="LBC Compress Prompt", value=LBC_COMPRESS_PROMPT),
        MultilineInput(
            name="instructions",
            display_name="LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS",
            value=LBC_DEFAULT_INSTRUCTIONS,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="compress")]

    def compress(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        field = _field(self.track)
        chunks = list(state.get("dc_output_{0}_chunks".format(field)) or [])
        query = state.get("query") or state.get("user_input") or ""

        if not self.enable_lbc:
            state["compressed_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = chunks
            state["lbc_validation_pairs_{0}".format(self.track)] = []
            self.status = "disabled - passing through {0} chunk(s)".format(len(chunks))
            return Data(data=state)

        if not chunks:
            state["compressed_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = []
            state["lbc_validation_pairs_{0}".format(self.track)] = []
            self.status = "no chunks"
            return Data(data=state)

        result = []
        pairs = []
        accepted = skipped = irrelevant_dropped = 0
        chars_before = sum(len(chunk.get("content", "")) for chunk in chunks)
        chars_after = 0

        for chunk in chunks:
            original = (chunk.get("content") or "").strip()
            if not original:
                result.append(chunk)
                skipped += 1
                continue

            prompt = self.compress_prompt.format(
                query=query,
                instructions=self.instructions,
                source=chunk.get("source", "?"),
                content=original,
            )
            parsed = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {}, repair_llm=self.repair_llm)
            if not isinstance(parsed, dict) or "compressed" not in parsed:
                result.append(chunk)
                chars_after += len(original)
                skipped += 1
                continue

            compressed = (parsed.get("compressed") or "").strip()

            if compressed == "__IRRELEVANT__":
                irrelevant_dropped += 1
                continue

            retention = len(compressed) / max(len(original), 1)
            if retention < self.min_retention_ratio:
                result.append(chunk)
                chars_after += len(original)
                skipped += 1
                continue

            if len(compressed) > len(original):
                # 'compressed' output expanded - over-expansion guard
                result.append(chunk)
                chars_after += len(original)
                skipped += 1
                continue

            if compressed == original:
                result.append(chunk)
                chars_after += len(original)
                skipped += 1
                continue

            compressed_chunk = {**chunk, "content": compressed}
            pairs.append({"original": chunk, "compressed": compressed_chunk})
            result.append(compressed_chunk)
            chars_after += len(compressed)
            accepted += 1

        state["compressed_{0}_chunks".format(field)] = result
        state["lbc_validation_pairs_{0}".format(self.track)] = pairs
        saved = chars_before - chars_after
        self.status = "{0} -> {1} chunk(s) ({2} dropped, {3} compressed, {4} kept) | chars {5} -> {6}".format(
            len(chunks), len(result), irrelevant_dropped, accepted, skipped, chars_before, chars_after
        ) + " (-{0})".format(saved)
        return Data(data=state)
'''


LBC_VALIDATOR = HEADER + r'''
LBC_JUDGE_PROMPT = __LBC_JUDGE_PROMPT__


class RagLbcValidator(Component):
    display_name = "validate_LBC_documents"
    description = (
        "validate_lbc_* in lbc.py. Judges every accepted compression for safety and records "
        "the verdicts. Always present so combine_tracks has a fixed predecessor, and a "
        "pass-through when ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION is off. Observational, like the "
        "LangGraph node - the compressed chunks are not modified."
    )
    icon = "shield-check"
    name = "RagLbcValidator"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Judge Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="judge_llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.0, 1024 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        DropdownInput(
            name="track",
            display_name="Track",
            options=["documents", "learned_qa"],
            value="documents",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="judge_prompt", display_name="LBC Judge Prompt", value=LBC_JUDGE_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="State", name="state_out", method="validate")]

    def validate(self) -> Data:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        pairs = list(state.get("lbc_validation_pairs_{0}".format(self.track)) or [])

        if not _switch(state, "ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION"):
            self.status = "compression validation disabled - passing through"
            return Data(data=state)
        if not pairs:
            self.status = "no compressed chunks to validate"
            return Data(data=state)

        verdicts = []
        for index, pair in enumerate(pairs):
            original = pair.get("original") or {}
            compressed = pair.get("compressed") or {}
            prompt = self.judge_prompt.format(
                query=state.get("query") or state.get("user_input") or "",
                source=original.get("source", "?"),
                original_content=original.get("content", ""),
                compressed_content=compressed.get("content", ""),
            )
            result = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {}, repair_llm=self.repair_llm)
            verdicts.append(
                {
                    "chunk": index,
                    "verdict": str((result or {}).get("verdict", "UNKNOWN")).upper()
                    if isinstance(result, dict) else "UNKNOWN",
                    "fabricated_claims": (result or {}).get("fabricated_claims") or []
                    if isinstance(result, dict) else [],
                    "lost_relevant_facts": (result or {}).get("lost_relevant_facts") or []
                    if isinstance(result, dict) else [],
                }
            )

        state["lbc_validation_{0}".format(self.track)] = verdicts
        self.status = "\n".join(
            "chunk {0}: verdict={1}  fabricated={2}  lost_relevant={3}".format(
                entry["chunk"], entry["verdict"], len(entry["fabricated_claims"]),
                len(entry["lost_relevant_facts"]),
            )
            for entry in verdicts
        )
        return Data(data=state)
'''


COMBINE_TRACKS = HEADER + r'''

class RagCombineTracks(Component):
    display_name = "combine_tracks"
    description = (
        "combine_tracks.py plus route_after_combine. Joins the two compression tracks with "
        "learned-QA chunks FIRST (higher precedence) and document chunks after, then routes "
        "to the draft, straight to the answer, back to a retry, or to no_context_answer."
    )
    icon = "merge"
    name = "RagCombineTracks"

    ROUTES = ("to_draft", "to_answer", "to_retry", "to_no_context")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="documents_state", display_name="Documents Track", is_list=True),
        DataInput(name="learned_qa_state", display_name="Learned-QA Track", is_list=True),
        IntInput(name="retry_limit", display_name="LLM_RESPONSE_RETRY_LIMIT", value=3, advanced=True),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Draft", name="to_draft", method="route_draft"),
        Output(display_name="To Answer", name="to_answer", method="route_answer"),
        Output(display_name="Retry", name="to_retry", method="route_retry"),
        Output(display_name="No Context", name="to_no_context", method="route_no_context"),
    ]

    def _combined(self):
        documents_state = _state_of(self.documents_state)
        learned_state = _state_of(self.learned_qa_state)
        state = dict(documents_state) or dict(learned_state)
        for key, value in learned_state.items():
            if key not in state or "learned_qa" in key:
                state[key] = value
        learned_qa = list(state.get("compressed_learned_qa_chunks") or [])
        documents = list(state.get("compressed_document_chunks") or [])
        # Learned-QA first: same precedence ordering as combine_tracks.py.
        state["compressed_docs"] = [*learned_qa, *documents]
        return state

    def _route(self, state):
        limit = int(state.get("retry_limit", self.retry_limit) or self.retry_limit)
        if not state.get("compressed_docs"):
            if int(state.get("retry_count", 0)) >= limit:
                return "to_no_context"
            return "to_retry"
        return "to_draft" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_ANSWER_DRAFT_CREATION") else "to_answer"

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._combined()
        route = self._route(state)
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "learned_qa={0}  documents={1}  combined={2}  retry_count={3}  route={4}".format(
            len(state.get("compressed_learned_qa_chunks") or []),
            len(state.get("compressed_document_chunks") or []),
            len(state.get("compressed_docs") or []),
            state.get("retry_count", 0),
            route,
        )
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_draft(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_draft")

    def route_answer(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_answer")

    def route_retry(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_retry")

    def route_no_context(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_no_context")
'''


GENERATE_DRAFT = HEADER + r'''
GENERATE_DRAFT_PROMPT = __GENERATE_DRAFT_PROMPT__


class RagGenerateDraft(Component):
    display_name = "generate_draft"
    description = (
        "generate_draft.py. Drafts an answer from compressed_docs against the original "
        "user_input, using the numbered context format the answer pipeline shares, then "
        "routes to the quality check or straight to generate_answer."
    )
    icon = "pencil-line"
    name = "RagGenerateDraft"

    ROUTES = ("to_quality", "to_answer")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="draft_prompt", display_name="Draft Prompt", value=GENERATE_DRAFT_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Quality Check", name="to_quality", method="route_quality"),
        Output(display_name="To Answer", name="to_answer", method="route_answer"),
    ]

    def _run(self):
        cached = getattr(self, "_drafted_state", None)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if not state.get("draft"):
            prompt = self.draft_prompt.format(
                query=state.get("user_input", ""),
                context=_build_context(state.get("compressed_docs") or []),
            )
            state["draft"] = _llm_text(self.llm, prompt)
        self._drafted_state = state
        return state

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._run()
        route = "to_quality" if _switch(state, "ENABLE_ANSWER_QUALITY_CHECK") else "to_answer"
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = state.get("draft", "")
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_quality(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_quality")

    def route_answer(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_answer")
'''


CHECK_ANSWER_QUALITY = HEADER + r'''
GROUNDING_PROMPT = __GROUNDING_PROMPT__


class RagCheckAnswerQuality(Component):
    display_name = "check_answer_quality"
    description = (
        "check_answer_quality.py. Grounding judge over the draft; any verdict outside the "
        "known set, an unparseable response or a failed call becomes UNKNOWN. GROUNDED "
        "continues to generate_answer, anything else retries while "
        "ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION is on and retry_count is under the limit, otherwise the "
        "best-effort draft is used."
    )
    icon = "scale"
    name = "RagCheckAnswerQuality"

    ROUTES = ("to_answer", "to_retry")
    PASS_VERDICT = "GROUNDED"
    VALID_VERDICTS = ("GROUNDED", "PARTIALLY_FABRICATED", "OVERCLAIMED", "OFF_TOPIC", "UNKNOWN")

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Judge Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="judge_llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.0, 1024 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="grounding_prompt", display_name="Grounding Prompt", value=GROUNDING_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="To Answer", name="to_answer", method="route_answer"),
        Output(display_name="Retry", name="to_retry", method="route_retry"),
    ]

    def _run(self):
        cached = getattr(self, "_checked_state", None)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if not state.get("quality_verdict"):
            prompt = self.grounding_prompt.format(
                query=state.get("user_input", ""),
                context=_build_context(state.get("compressed_docs") or []),
                answer=state.get("draft", ""),
            )
            result = _parse_json(_llm_text(self.llm, prompt), {}, repair_llm=self.repair_llm)
            if isinstance(result, dict) and result:
                verdict = str(result.get("verdict") or "UNKNOWN").upper().strip()
                if verdict not in self.VALID_VERDICTS:
                    verdict = "UNKNOWN"
                state["quality_verdict"] = verdict
                state["quality_reason"] = str(result.get("overall_reason") or "")
                state["quality_unsupported_claims"] = result.get("unsupported_claims") or []
            else:
                state["quality_verdict"] = "UNKNOWN"
                state["quality_reason"] = "judge response was not valid JSON"
        self._checked_state = state
        return state

    def _route(self, state):
        if state.get("quality_verdict") == self.PASS_VERDICT:
            return "to_answer"
        limit = int(state.get("retry_limit", 3) or 3)
        budget_ok = _switch(state, "ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION") and int(state.get("retry_count", 0)) < limit
        return "to_retry" if budget_ok else "to_answer"

    def _emit(self, target):
        state = self._run()
        route = self._route(state)
        if route != target:
            self.stop(target)
            return Data(data={})
        for other in self.ROUTES:
            if other != target:
                self.stop(other)
        self.status = "verdict={0} unsupported_claims={1} route={2}\n{3}".format(
            state.get("quality_verdict"),
            len(state.get("quality_unsupported_claims") or []),
            route,
            state.get("quality_reason", ""),
        )
        return Data(data=state)

    def route_answer(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_answer")

    def route_retry(self) -> Data:
        return self._emit("to_retry")
'''


GENERATE_ANSWER = HEADER + r'''
GENERATE_ANSWER_PROMPT = __GENERATE_ANSWER_PROMPT__

GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT = __GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT__


class RagGenerateAnswer(Component):
    description = (
        "generate_answer.py. Uses the from-draft prompt when a draft exists and the direct "
        "prompt otherwise; if the final synthesis call fails it falls back to the draft "
        "rather than returning nothing. Quality is derived from the judge verdict. Always "
        "emits the answer; the distillation branch is taken only when "
        "ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION is on."
    )
    display_name = "generate_answer"
    icon = "message-square"
    name = "RagGenerateAnswer"

    PASS_VERDICT = "GROUNDED"

    inputs = [
        DataInput(name="state", display_name="State", is_list=True),
        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="answer_prompt", display_name="Answer Prompt", value=GENERATE_ANSWER_PROMPT),
        MultilineInput(
            name="answer_from_draft_prompt",
            display_name="Answer From Draft Prompt",
            value=GENERATE_ANSWER_FROM_DRAFT_PROMPT,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [
        Output(display_name="Answer", name="answer", method="build_answer"),
        Output(display_name="To Distillation", name="to_distill", method="route_distillation"),
    ]

    def _run(self):
        cached = getattr(self, "_answer_state", None)
        if cached is not None:
            return cached
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if not state.get("answer"):
            user_input = state.get("user_input", "")
            context = _build_context(state.get("compressed_docs") or [])
            draft = state.get("draft")
            if draft:
                prompt = self.answer_from_draft_prompt.format(
                    query=user_input, context=context, draft=draft
                )
            else:
                prompt = self.answer_prompt.format(query=user_input, context=context)
            try:
                answer = _llm_text(self.llm, prompt)
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - mirrors the not-ok branch in generate_answer.py
                answer = ""
            if not answer and draft:
                # Final synthesis failed - fall back to the draft rather than nothing.
                answer = draft
            state["answer"] = answer

            verdict = str(state.get("quality_verdict") or "").upper().strip()
            state["quality"] = "OK" if (not verdict or verdict == self.PASS_VERDICT) else verdict
            state["sources"] = [
                {"source": chunk.get("source", "?")} for chunk in (state.get("compressed_docs") or [])
            ]
        self._answer_state = state
        return state

    def build_answer(self) -> Message:
        state = self._run()
        self.status = "quality={0}\n\n{1}".format(state.get("quality", "OK"), state.get("answer", ""))
        return Message(text=state.get("answer", ""))

    def route_distillation(self) -> Data:
        state = self._run()
        if not _switch(state, "ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION", default=False):
            self.stop("to_distill")
            return Data(data={})
        return Data(data=state)
'''

class RagAutoDistillation(Component):
    display_name = "auto_distillation"

    )
    icon = "graduation-cap"
    name = "RagAutoDistillation"

    inputs = [

        HandleInput(
            name="llm",
            display_name="Generation Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=True,
            info="llm in llm_setup.py: temperature 0.1, 2048 output tokens.",
        ),
        HandleInput(
            name="repair_llm",
            display_name="JSON Repair Model",
            input_types=["LanguageModel"],
            required=False,
            info="Groq client used only to repair malformed JSON (temperature 0.0, 1024 tokens).",
        ),
        MultilineInput(name="distill_prompt", display_name="Distill Prompt", value=DISTILL_PROMPT),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="QA Pairs", name="qa_pairs", method="distill")]

    def distill(self) -> Message:
        state = _state_of(self.state)
        if not state:
            return Message(text="")
        prompt = self.distill_prompt.format(
            query=state.get("user_input", ""),
            answer=state.get("answer", ""),
            chunks=_chunks_block(state.get("compressed_docs") or []),
        )

        text = json.dumps(pairs, indent=2)
        self.status = text
        return Message(text=text)

"""Run every generated component against the semantics of app_workflow/nodes/*.py.

Uses fake Groq / Hugging Face / Knowledge / Memory Base clients so the whole pipeline
can be exercised without a key, a knowledge base or a memory base.
"""

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from gen_flow import COMPONENT_SOURCES, inject  # noqa: E402  (also regenerates the flow)
from lfx.schema.data import Data
from lfx.schema.message import Message


class FakeResponse:
    def __init__(self, content):
        self.content = content


class FakeLLM:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []
        self.variant_calls = 0

    def invoke(self, prompt):
        self.calls.append(prompt)
        lowered = prompt.lower()
        if "query-reformulation" in lowered:
            # A retry must propose phrasings that were not blocked by the previous pass.
            self.variant_calls += 1
            suffix = "" if self.variant_calls == 1 else f" round {self.variant_calls}"
            return FakeResponse(
                f'[{{"query": "asd motor speed{suffix}"}}, {{"query": "vfd energy savings{suffix}"}},'
                f' {{"query": "drive control basics{suffix}"}}]'
            )
        if "relevance judge for a retrieval system" in lowered:
            # Only chunk 1 is explicitly irrelevant; anything unmentioned must survive.
            return FakeResponse(
                '{"verdict": "PARTIAL", "per_chunk": [{"index": 1, "relevant": false, "reason": "off topic"}],'
                ' "overall_reason": "thin"}'
            )
        if "you merge near-duplicate" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('{"content": "merged text.", "sources": ["a.pdf", "b.pdf"], "merged_from": 2}')
        if "faithfulness judge" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('{"fabricated_claims": [], "dropped_claims": [], "overall_reason": "faithful"}')
        if "sentence-level redundancy annotation" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse(
                '[{"members": [{"chunk_index": 1, "sentence": "Shared fact one."},'
                ' {"chunk_index": 0, "sentence": "Shared fact one."}]},'
                ' {"members": [{"chunk_index": 2, "sentence": "Intra one."},'
                ' {"chunk_index": 2, "sentence": "Intra two."}]}]'
            )
        if "validating proposed redundancy groups" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('[{"group_index": 0, "verdict": "CONFIRMED", "reason": "same fact"}]')
        if "query-focused chunk compressor" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse(
                '{"compressed": "Kept one. Kept two.", "dropped_count": 1, "reason": "r"}'
            )
        if "compression-safety judge" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse(
                '{"verdict": "SAFE", "fabricated_claims": [], "lost_relevant_facts": [], "overall_reason": "ok"}'
            )
        if "strict quality judge" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse(
                '{"verdict": "GROUNDED", "unsupported_claims": [], "scope_mismatch": "", "overall_reason": "ok"}'
            )
        if "knowledge distillation assistant" in lowered:
            return FakeResponse('[{"question": "q1", "answer": "a1"}]')
        return FakeResponse("A short grounded answer.")


class FakeEmbeddings:
    """Deterministic bag-of-words vectors - enough to exercise the cosine paths."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = 0

    def embed_documents(self, texts):
        self.calls += 1
        vocabulary = sorted({word for text in texts for word in text.lower().split()})
        return [
            [float(text.lower().split().count(word)) for word in vocabulary] or [0.0]
            for text in texts
        ]


class FakeDataFrame(list):
    """Stands in for the DataFrame the Knowledge / Memory Base nodes emit."""

    def __init__(self, rows):
        super().__init__(rows)
        self.columns = list(rows[0].keys()) if rows else []

    def to_dict(self, orient="records"):
        return [dict(row) for row in self]


CLASSES = {}
CODE = {}
for name, source in COMPONENT_SOURCES.items():
    namespace = {}
    code = inject(source)
    exec(code, namespace)  # noqa: S102 - this is the code Langflow will execute
    CODE[name] = code
    CLASSES[name] = next(
        value
        for value in namespace.values()
        if isinstance(value, type) and getattr(value, "name", None) == name
    )


def make(name, **overrides):
    """Instantiate a component with its declared defaults, then apply overrides."""
    cls = CLASSES[name]
    instance = cls(_code=CODE[name])
    stopped = []
    instance.stop = stopped.append
    instance._stopped = stopped
    for field in cls.inputs:
        object.__setattr__(instance, field.name, getattr(field, "value", None))
    for key, value in overrides.items():
        object.__setattr__(instance, key, value)
    return instance


def state_of(result):
    return dict(result.data)


failures = []


def check(label, condition, detail=""):
    status = "ok " if condition else "FAIL"
    print(f"  [{status}] {label}{(' - ' + detail) if detail and not condition else ''}")
    if not condition:
        failures.append(label)


llm = FakeLLM()
embeddings = FakeEmbeddings()

memory_rows = FakeDataFrame(
    [
        {"content": "Earlier answer about drives.", "_score": 0.7, "session_id": "s1"},
        {
            "content": "[thumbdown] answer missed the energy-saving part",
            "_score": 0.6,
            "session_id": "s1",
            "quality": "USER_THUMBSDOWN",
            "user_feedback": "you never mentioned energy savings",
            "original_query": "How do ASDs control motor speed?",
            "variants": "asd speed control | motor rpm drive",
        },
        {"content": "note", "_score": 0.4, "session_id": "s2", "failed_variants": "vfd basics"},
    ]
)

print("\n== user_input (user_input.py) ==")
node = make(
    "RagUserInput",
    chat_message=Message(text="How do ASDs control motor speed and save energy?"),
    prior_memory=memory_rows,
)
query_state = state_of(node.route_query())
check("query branch carries state", bool(query_state.get("query")))
check("command branches stopped", set(node._stopped) == {"to_stats", "to_learn", "to_bad", "to_exit"})
check("retry_count starts at 0", query_state["retry_count"] == 0)
check("thumbdown parsed", len(query_state["prior_thumbdowns"]) == 1)
check(
    "blocked expanded with thumbdown reformulations",
    query_state["blocked_variants"] == ["vfd basics", "asd speed control", "motor rpm drive"],
    str(query_state["blocked_variants"]),
)
for command, output in (("quit", "route_exit"), ("exit", "route_exit"), ("stats", "route_stats")):
    probe = make("RagUserInput", chat_message=Message(text=command))
    check(f"{command!r} takes the command branch", bool(state_of(getattr(probe, output)())))

print("\n== cmd_* (commands.py) ==")
node = make("RagCommand", state=Data(data=query_state), memory=memory_rows, command="stats")
check("cmd reports memory rows", "Memory rows       : 3" in node.run_command().text)

print("\n== generate_query_variants (query_variants.py) ==")
node = make("RagGenerateQueryVariants", state=Data(data=query_state), llm=llm, embeddings=embeddings)
variant_state = state_of(node.build_variants())
check("retry_count increments on the first pass", variant_state["retry_count"] == 1)
check(
    "original query is not injected as a variant",
    query_state["query"] not in variant_state["query_variants"],
    str(variant_state["query_variants"]),
)
check(
    "capped to the adaptive branch budget",
    len(variant_state["query_variants"]) <= variant_state["branch_budget"],
    f'{variant_state["query_variants"]} budget={variant_state["branch_budget"]}',
)
check("embeddings used by pre_retrieval_filter", embeddings.calls > 0)

budgets = {}
for text, expected in (
    ("what is asd", "short"),          # <= 4 words, no conjunction  -> SHORT
    ("how does an asd work", "medium"),  # has 'how'                  -> MEDIUM (<= 8 words)
    ("explain and compare adjustable speed drives against soft starters for pumps", "conjunction"),
):
    probe = make(
        "RagGenerateQueryVariants",
        state=Data(data=dict(query_state, user_input=text, query=text)),
        llm=llm,
        embeddings=embeddings,
    )
    budgets[expected] = state_of(probe.build_variants())["branch_budget"]
check("short question budget = 2", budgets["short"] == 2, str(budgets))
check("medium question budget = 2", budgets["medium"] == 2, str(budgets))
check("conjunction question budget = 3", budgets["conjunction"] == 3, str(budgets))

disabled = dict(query_state)
disabled["switches"] = dict(disabled["switches"], ENABLE_SUB_QUERY_GENERATION=False)
probe = make("RagGenerateQueryVariants", state=Data(data=disabled), llm=llm, embeddings=embeddings)
off_state = state_of(probe.build_variants())
check("disabled path still increments retry_count", off_state["retry_count"] == 1)
check("disabled path uses the user query", off_state["query_variants"] == [disabled["user_input"]])

node = make("RagGenerateQueryVariants", state=Data(data=query_state), llm=llm, embeddings=embeddings)
first = state_of(node.build_variants())
check("pass 1 searches variant 1", node.build_search_query().text == first["query_variants"][0])

node = make("RagGenerateQueryVariants", state=Data(data=first), llm=llm, embeddings=embeddings)
retry_state = state_of(node.build_variants())
check("retry increments retry_count to 2", retry_state["retry_count"] == 2)
check("previous variants blocked on retry", all(
    variant in retry_state["blocked_variants"] for variant in first["query_variants"]))
check(
    "pass 2 searches variant 2",
    node.build_search_query().text == retry_state["query_variants"][1],
    node.build_search_query().text,
)

print("\n== retrieve (retrieve.py) ==")
knowledge_rows = FakeDataFrame(
    [
        {"content": "Shared fact one. ASDs vary motor speed.", "_score": 0.88,
         "source": "a.pdf", "chunk_seq": 0},
        {"content": "Shared fact one. Drives lower electricity costs.", "_score": 0.74,
         "source": "a.pdf", "chunk_seq": 1},
        {"content": "Intra one. Intra two. An unrelated paragraph about invoices for the "
                    "accounting department that runs long.", "_score": 0.61,
         "source": "b.pdf", "chunk_seq": 7},
        {"content": "Below the retrieval floor.", "_score": 0.20, "source": "c.pdf"},
    ]
)
learned_rows = FakeDataFrame(
    [{"content": "Q: what is an ASD? A: an adjustable speed drive.", "_score": 0.71}]
)
node = make(
    "RagRetrieve",
    state=Data(data=first),
    documents_results=knowledge_rows,
    learned_qa_results=learned_rows,
)
retrieved = state_of(node.retrieve())
documents = retrieved["retrieved_document_chunks"]
check("below-threshold row dropped by DOCUMENTS_MIN_SIMILARITY", len(documents) == 3, str(len(documents)))
check("flat chunk shape uses similarity_score", documents[0]["similarity_score"] == 0.88)
check("chunk_seq lifted to the top level as an int", documents[0]["chunk_seq"] == 0)
check("learned_qa track kept", len(retrieved["retrieved_learned_qa_chunks"]) == 1)
check("variant entry recorded", retrieved["variants_with_chunks"][0]["query"] == first["query_variants"][0])

node = make("RagRetrieve", state=Data(data=first))
check("empty knowledge result marks the variant failed",
      state_of(node.retrieve())["newly_failed_variants"] != [])

print("\n== post_retrieval_filter (post_retrieve.py) ==")
node = make("RagPostRetrievalFilter", state=Data(data=retrieved))
filtered = state_of(node.filter_chunks())
check("single variant passes everything through",
      len(filtered["post_filtered_document_chunks"]) == 3)

duplicated = dict(retrieved)
duplicated["variants_with_chunks"] = retrieved["variants_with_chunks"] + [
    {
        "query": "a redundant reformulation",
        "document_chunks": retrieved["variants_with_chunks"][0]["document_chunks"],
        "learned_qa_chunks": retrieved["variants_with_chunks"][0]["learned_qa_chunks"],
    }
]
node = make("RagPostRetrievalFilter", state=Data(data=duplicated))
deduped = state_of(node.filter_chunks())
check("redundant variant detected", "redundant_variants=1" in node.status, node.status)
check("kept-variant chunks survive", len(deduped["post_filtered_document_chunks"]) == 3)

print("\n== validate_retrieval (validate_retrieval.py) ==")
node = make("RagRetrievalValidator", state=Data(data=filtered), llm=llm, track="documents")
validated = state_of(node.validate())
check(
    "only explicit relevant=false chunks are dropped",
    len(validated["validated_document_chunks"]) == 2,
    str(len(validated["validated_document_chunks"])),
)
check("verdict recorded", validated["document_retrieval_verdict"] == "PARTIAL")

unknown = make(
    "RagRetrievalValidator",
    state=Data(data=filtered),
    llm=type("L", (), {"invoke": lambda self, p: FakeResponse("not json at all")})(),
    track="documents",
)

      len(state_of(unknown.validate())["validated_document_chunks"]) == 3)

node = make("RagRetrievalValidator", state=Data(data=validated), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("learned_qa track validated", len(validated["validated_learned_qa_chunks"]) == 1)

print("\n== dedup_merge (dedup_merge.py) ==")
node = make("RagDedupMerge", state=Data(data=validated), llm=llm, embeddings=embeddings, track="documents")
merged = state_of(node.merge())
check("merge pairs keyed source_chunks",
      all("source_chunks" in pair for pair in merged["dedup_merge_document_pairs"]))
check("surviving chunks cleaned to the flat shape",
      all(set(chunk) <= {"content", "source", "similarity_score", "chunk_seq"}
          for chunk in merged["dedup_merged_document_chunks"]),
      str(merged["dedup_merged_document_chunks"]))
node = make("RagDedupMerge", state=Data(data=merged), llm=llm, embeddings=embeddings, track="learned_qa")
merged = state_of(node.merge())
check("learned_qa dedup-merge ran", "dedup_merged_learned_qa_chunks" in merged)

node = make("RagMergeValidator", state=Data(data=merged), llm=llm, track="documents")

check("chunks are not modified by the validator",
      merge_validated["dedup_merged_document_chunks"] == merged["dedup_merged_document_chunks"])

merge_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("learned_qa merge validator ran", "dedup_merged_learned_qa_chunks" in merge_validated)

print("\n== NAC_documents (nac.py) ==")
nac_seed = dict(merge_validated)
nac_seed["dedup_merged_document_chunks"] = [
    {"content": "Body one.", "source": "a.pdf", "chunk_seq": 1},
    {"content": "Body three.", "source": "a.pdf", "chunk_seq": 3},
    {"content": "Body two.", "source": "a.pdf", "chunk_seq": 2},
    {"content": "Other doc.", "source": "b.pdf", "chunk_seq": 9},

check("merged run carries chunk_seq_start/end",
      merged_chunk is not None and merged_chunk["chunk_seq_start"] == 1
      and merged_chunk["chunk_seq_end"] == 3, str(merged_chunk))
check("merged run drops chunk_seq", merged_chunk is not None and "chunk_seq" not in merged_chunk)
check("chunk without chunk_seq passes through",
      any(chunk.get("source") == "c.pdf" for chunk in output))
check("nac routed to validator", node._stopped == ["to_dc"])

no_seq = dict(nac_seed)
no_seq["dedup_merged_document_chunks"] = [{"content": "x", "source": "a.pdf"}]

      state_of(probe.route_validator())["nac_output_document_chunks"] == no_seq["dedup_merged_document_chunks"])

print("\n== validate_NAC_documents (nac.py) ==")

      nac_validated["nac_output_document_chunks"] == nac["nac_output_document_chunks"])

print("\n== DC (dc.py) ==")
dc_seed = dict(nac_validated)
dc_seed["nac_output_document_chunks"] = [
    {"content": "Shared fact one. Unique to chunk zero, which is long enough to survive the guard.",
     "source": "a.pdf"},
    {"content": "Shared fact one. Unique to chunk one, also long enough to survive the length guard.",
     "source": "b.pdf"},
    {"content": "Intra one. Intra two. Filler text that keeps this chunk comfortably above the floor.",
     "source": "c.pdf"},
]
node = make("RagDcCompress", state=Data(data=dc_seed), llm=llm, track="documents")
dc = state_of(node.route_validator())
chunks_out = dc["dc_output_document_chunks"]
check("lowest chunk index keeps the shared sentence",
      "Shared fact one." in chunks_out[0]["content"], chunks_out[0]["content"])
check("higher chunk index loses it",
      "Shared fact one." not in chunks_out[1]["content"], chunks_out[1]["content"])
check("intra-chunk group ignored",
      "Intra one." in chunks_out[2]["content"] and "Intra two." in chunks_out[2]["content"])
check("window structure carries window_chunks",
      all("window_chunks" in entry for entry in dc["dc_groups_per_window_documents"]))

short_seed = dict(dc_seed)
short_seed["nac_output_document_chunks"] = [
    {"content": "Shared fact one. Tiny.", "source": "a.pdf"},
    {"content": "Shared fact one. Also tiny.", "source": "b.pdf"},
]

check("removal that would leave < 50 chars is skipped",
      short_out[1]["content"] == "Shared fact one. Also tiny.", short_out[1]["content"])

node = make("RagDcCompress", state=Data(data=dc), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
dc = state_of(node.route_validator())
check("learned_qa DC ran", "dc_output_learned_qa_chunks" in dc)

print("\n== validate_DC (dc.py) ==")
node = make("RagDcValidator", state=Data(data=dc), llm=llm, track="documents")
dc_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("dc validator leaves chunks untouched",
      dc_validated["dc_output_document_chunks"] == dc["dc_output_document_chunks"])

print("\n== LBC (lbc.py) ==")
lbc_seed = dict(dc_validated)
lbc_seed["dc_output_document_chunks"] = [
    {"content": "Kept one. Kept two. Dropped three which makes this chunk long enough.",
     "source": "a.pdf"},
    {"content": "Kept one. Kept two.", "source": "b.pdf"},  # compressor returns the same text
    {"content": "Kept one. Kept two. " + ("padding sentence. " * 12), "source": "c.pdf"},  # over-compression
]
node = make("RagLbcCompress", state=Data(data=lbc_seed), llm=llm, track="documents")
lbc = state_of(node.compress())
compressed = lbc["compressed_document_chunks"]
check("all three chunks survive", len(compressed) == 3, str(len(compressed)))
check("genuine compression accepted", compressed[0]["content"] == "Kept one. Kept two.")
check("no-change chunk kept as-is and not paired", compressed[1] == lbc_seed["dc_output_document_chunks"][1])
check("over-compression guard keeps the original",
      compressed[2]["content"] == lbc_seed["dc_output_document_chunks"][2]["content"])
check("only real compressions become validation pairs",
      len(lbc["lbc_validation_pairs_documents"]) == 1, str(len(lbc["lbc_validation_pairs_documents"])))

irrelevant = make(
    "RagLbcCompress",
    state=Data(data=lbc_seed),
    llm=type("L", (), {"invoke": lambda self, p: FakeResponse('{"compressed": "__IRRELEVANT__"}')})(),
    track="documents",
)
check("__IRRELEVANT__ drops the chunk",
      state_of(irrelevant.compress())["compressed_document_chunks"] == [])

node = make("RagLbcCompress", state=Data(data=lbc), llm=llm, track="learned_qa")
lbc = state_of(node.compress())
check("learned_qa LBC ran", "compressed_learned_qa_chunks" in lbc)

print("\n== validate_LBC (lbc.py) ==")
node = make("RagLbcValidator", state=Data(data=lbc), llm=llm, track="documents")
lbc_validated = state_of(node.validate())
check("lbc validator leaves chunks untouched",
      lbc_validated["compressed_document_chunks"] == lbc["compressed_document_chunks"])

off = dict(lbc)
off["switches"] = dict(off["switches"], ENABLE_COMPRESSION_VALIDATION=False)
probe = make("RagLbcValidator", state=Data(data=off), llm=llm, track="documents")
probe.validate()
check("lbc validator is a pass-through when disabled", "disabled" in probe.status)

print("\n== combine_tracks (combine_tracks.py) ==")
combine_seed = dict(lbc_validated)
combine_seed["compressed_document_chunks"] = [{"content": "doc chunk", "source": "a.pdf"}]
combine_seed["compressed_learned_qa_chunks"] = [{"content": "learned chunk", "source": "learned_qa"}]
node = make(
    "RagCombineTracks",
    documents_state=[Data(data=combine_seed)],
    learned_qa_state=[Data(data=combine_seed)],
)
combined = state_of(node.route_draft())
check("learned_qa chunks come first",
      combined["compressed_docs"][0]["content"] == "learned chunk",
      str(combined["compressed_docs"]))
check("documents follow", combined["compressed_docs"][1]["content"] == "doc chunk")
check("draft branch taken", set(node._stopped) == {"to_answer", "to_retry", "to_no_context"})

empty = dict(combine_seed, compressed_document_chunks=[], compressed_learned_qa_chunks=[])
node = make("RagCombineTracks", documents_state=[Data(data=empty)], learned_qa_state=[Data(data=empty)])
check("empty context retries", bool(state_of(node.route_retry())))
exhausted = dict(empty, retry_count=3)
node = make("RagCombineTracks", documents_state=[Data(data=exhausted)], learned_qa_state=[Data(data=exhausted)])
check("exhausted retries -> no_context", bool(state_of(node.route_no_context())))

print("\n== generate_draft (generate_draft.py) ==")
node = make("RagGenerateDraft", state=Data(data=combined), llm=llm)
drafted = state_of(node.route_quality())
check("draft written", bool(drafted.get("draft")))
check("context is numbered and source-free",
      "[1] learned chunk" in node.llm.calls[-1] and "[Source:" not in node.llm.calls[-1])
check("draft prompt uses user_input", combined["user_input"] in node.llm.calls[-1])

print("\n== check_answer_quality (check_answer_quality.py) ==")
node = make("RagCheckAnswerQuality", state=Data(data=drafted), llm=llm)
checked = state_of(node.route_answer())
check("verdict recorded", checked["quality_verdict"] == "GROUNDED")
check("retry branch stopped", node._stopped == ["to_retry"])

bogus = make(
    "RagCheckAnswerQuality",
    state=Data(data=drafted),
    llm=type("L", (), {"invoke": lambda self, p: FakeResponse('{"verdict": "SPLENDID"}')})(),
)
check("unknown verdict normalised to UNKNOWN",
      state_of(bogus.route_retry())["quality_verdict"] == "UNKNOWN")

no_budget = dict(drafted, retry_count=3)
probe = make(
    "RagCheckAnswerQuality",
    state=Data(data=no_budget),
    llm=type("L", (), {"invoke": lambda self, p: FakeResponse('{"verdict": "OVERCLAIMED"}')})(),
)
check("no budget left -> best-effort answer", bool(state_of(probe.route_answer())))

print("\n== generate_answer (generate_answer.py) ==")
node = make("RagGenerateAnswer", state=Data(data=checked), llm=llm)
check("answer message produced", bool(node.build_answer().text))
check("quality derived from the verdict", state_of(node.route_distillation() or Data(data={})) is not None)
check("distillation branch stopped by default", state_of(node.route_distillation()) == {})


class FailingLLM:
    def invoke(self, prompt):
        raise RuntimeError("groq is down")


node = make("RagGenerateAnswer", state=Data(data=checked), llm=FailingLLM())
check("failed synthesis falls back to the draft", node.build_answer().text == checked["draft"])

overclaimed = dict(checked, quality_verdict="OVERCLAIMED", answer="")
node = make("RagGenerateAnswer", state=Data(data=overclaimed), llm=llm)
node.build_answer()
check("non-GROUNDED verdict becomes the quality label", "quality=OVERCLAIMED" in node.status, node.status)

distil_state = dict(checked, answer="")
distil_state["switches"] = dict(distil_state["switches"], ENABLE_AUTO_DISTILLATION=True)
node = make("RagGenerateAnswer", state=Data(data=distil_state), llm=llm)
node.build_answer()
check("distillation branch taken when enabled", bool(state_of(node.route_distillation())))

print("\n== auto_distillation (auto_distillation.py) ==")
node = make("RagAutoDistillation", state=Data(data=distil_state), llm=llm)
check("qa pairs emitted", json.loads(node.distill().text)[0]["question"] == "q1")

print("\n== no_context_answer (no_context_answer.py) ==")
node = make("RagNoContextAnswer", state=Data(data=exhausted))
check("no-context message emitted", "knowledge base does not contain" in node.build_answer().text)
check("logged as INSUFFICIENT", "quality=INSUFFICIENT" in node.status)

print("\n== JSON repair client (fix_llm_output.py) ==")


class BrokenJSONLLM:
    def invoke(self, prompt):
        return FakeResponse('{"verdict": "PASS", "per_chunk": [{"index": 0, "relevant": true,')


class RepairLLM:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = 0

    def invoke(self, prompt):
        self.calls += 1
        return FakeResponse('{"verdict": "PASS", "per_chunk": []}')


repair = RepairLLM()
node = make(
    "RagRetrievalValidator",
    state=Data(data=filtered),
    llm=BrokenJSONLLM(),
    repair_llm=repair,
    track="documents",
)
repaired = state_of(node.validate())
check("repair client called on malformed JSON", repair.calls == 1)
check("verdict recovered after repair", repaired["document_retrieval_verdict"] == "PASS")

node = make("RagRetrievalValidator", state=Data(data=filtered), llm=BrokenJSONLLM(), track="documents")
check("unrepairable JSON degrades safely", "validated_document_chunks" in state_of(node.validate()))

print("\n" + ("ALL CHECKS PASSED" if not failures else f"{len(failures)} FAILURES: {failures}"))
sys.exit(1 if failures else 0)

cd "c:/Users/LOQ/Desktop/Projects/Langflow-learning-project" && .venv/Scripts/python.exe "/c/Users/LOQ/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/c--Users-LOQ-Desktop-Projects-Langflow-learning-project/50ed6fa8-f1bd-4671-8c9a-9b0e005af782/scratchpad/test_components.py" 2>&1 | grep -c "\[ok \]"

OUT
87
No — it was not working accordingly. Auditing against the real node bodies turned up 13 genuine divergences. I fixed all of them and regenerated the flow.

Behavioural bugs found
combine_tracks — I concatenated documents first. combine_tracks.py puts learned-QA first (higher precedence). Every downstream prompt saw the context in the wrong order.

retry_count — I only incremented on re-entry. query_variants.py increments on every pass including the disabled path, so the first pass is already retry_count == 1. My version gave the graph one extra retry.

generate_query_variants — four separate errors: it read state["query"] instead of user_input; it prepended the original query as variant 0 (the real node never does — variants come purely from the model, with the query only as an empty-list fallback); adaptive_branch_budget used a word-set intersection plus max() instead of the substring test and if/elif/else chain; and pre_retrieval_filter was missing entirely — inter-variant cosine dedup at PRE_RETRIEVAL_SIM_THRESHOLD, then ranking survivors against the user query and capping to budget. The system prompt was also a stub, missing the BLOCKED VARIANTS block, the USER-FLAGGED PRIOR FAILURE history (with per-variant retrieved chunks), and the trailing PRIORITY-FEEDBACK block.

post_retrieval_filter — I'd invented it. The real node drops redundant variants (variants whose combined (content, source) set matches an earlier one) and filters the accumulated lists to kept-variant chunks. The similarity floors I'd put here actually belong in retrieve; that's where they are now.

validate_retrieval — I kept only chunks the judge explicitly marked relevant. The real node drops only explicit relevant: false, so unmentioned chunks survive and UNKNOWN keeps everything. Mine was silently discarding context.

Chunk shape — I used score with chunk_seq nested in metadata. The real _flatten_chunks produces similarity_score and a top-level int chunk_seq. NAC's run detection keys on exactly that, so my NAC could never have fired on real data.

NAC — didn't sort by (source, chunk_seq), used abs(diff) == 1 instead of strict prev + 1, never set chunk_seq_start/chunk_seq_end, and didn't append the no-chunk_seq chunks at the end.

DC — the keeper is the lowest chunk index, not the first listed member; groups must span two distinct chunks (my version was applying intra-chunk repetition groups); and the no-change and 50-character guards were missing, as was [Source: …] stripping before the scan.

LBC — missing the over-expansion guard and the identical-output case, so both were being recorded as validation pairs.

All four validator families — mine mutated the pipeline (reverting merges, restoring sentences, restoring chunks). Every real validator returns {} and only logs. They're observational now; verdicts are recorded on the state so they still surface on the canvas.

_build_context — draft/quality/answer use [1] content, not the [Source: …]-tagged judge block I was passing.

generate_answer — no draft fallback when the synthesis call fails, and no quality label derived from the verdict.

user_input — didn't expand blocked_variants with every reformulation recorded on a prior thumbdown.

Verification
87 checks now pass, written against the node sources rather than my reading of them — including the exact budget thresholds (2/2/3), the DC keeper and guards, the three LBC guards, UNKNOWN keeping all chunks, NAC run boundaries, and validator non-mutation. Two test fixtures were themselves wrong and the guards caught them, which is a good sign. Graph.from_payload still loads the flow: 45 vertices, 98 edges, cycles intact.

What still differs, by platform constraint
One variant searched per pass. LangGraph issues a Send per variant and accumulates via operator.add. Langflow fans out on edges, so the Knowledge nodes run once per pass and each retry advances to the next variant. The redundant-variant dedup in post_retrieval_filter is implemented in full but only bites once more than one variant entry accumulates.
No feedback write-back. feedback_store.log() / save_failed_variants() have no Memory Base equivalent — Langflow captures the turn itself. Quality and failed variants are still computed and shown on the node.
auto_distillation runs the distillation prompt directly instead of calling self_learner.run_distillation().
All three are on the canvas note. If you want the fan-out to be real, the honest fix is a Loop component around the retrieval segment — that's a structural change worth deciding on deliberately.

Knowledge · learned_qa is missing Knowledge.
Memory Base (feedback + prior turns) is missing Memory Base."

    def load_directory(self) -> list[Data]:
        path = self.path
        types = self.types
        depth = self.depth
        max_concurrency = self.max_concurrency
        load_hidden = self.load_hidden
        recursive = self.recursive
        silent_errors = self.silent_errors
        use_multithreading = self.use_multithreading

        resolved_path = self._resolve_directory_path(path)

        # If no types are specified, use all supported types
        if not types:
            types = TEXT_FILE_TYPES

        # Check if all specified types are valid
        invalid_types = [t for t in types if t not in TEXT_FILE_TYPES]
        if invalid_types:
            msg = f"Invalid file types specified: {invalid_types}. Valid types are: {TEXT_FILE_TYPES}"
            raise ValueError(msg)

        valid_types = types

        file_paths = retrieve_file_paths(
            resolved_path, load_hidden=load_hidden, recursive=recursive, depth=depth, types=valid_types
        )

        loaded_data = []
        if use_multithreading:
            loaded_data = parallel_load_data(file_paths, silent_errors=silent_errors, max_concurrency=max_concurrency)
        else:
            loaded_data = [parse_text_file_to_data(file_path, silent_errors=silent_errors) for file_path in file_paths]

        valid_data = [x for x in loaded_data if x is not None and isinstance(x, Data)]
        self.status = valid_data
        return valid_data

"""Custom-component source for the ingestion pipeline.

Mirrors the /ingest flow in api.py -> ingestion.ingestion_requests.run_ingestion, whose
switches are ENABLE_MARKER_LOADER and ENABLE_CUSTOM_SPLITTER (config.py). The vector
store write is done by a Knowledge node in Ingest mode rather than VectorStore.
"""

from flow_components_a import HEADER

MARKER_LOADER = HEADER + r'''

class RagMarkerLoader(Component):
    display_name = "marker_loader"
    description = (
        "ENABLE_MARKER_LOADER. Marker runs in an isolated GPU container (marker_service/); "
        "this posts each PDF to MARKER_SERVICE_URL and replaces the file's text with the "
        "Markdown it returns, so the custom splitter can use the heading structure. When the "
        "switch is off every row passes through with the text Langflow's Directory node "
        "already extracted (the Unstructured path)."
    )
    icon = "file-scan"
    name = "RagMarkerLoader"

    inputs = [
        HandleInput(
            name="files",
            display_name="Files",
            input_types=["DataFrame", "Table", "Data"],
            is_list=True,
            required=True,
            info="Rows from the Directory node: one per discovered file.",
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_marker_loader",
            display_name="ENABLE_MARKER_LOADER",
            value=False,
            info="False keeps Langflow's own text extraction; True converts PDFs with Marker.",
        ),
        StrInput(
            name="marker_service_url",
            display_name="MARKER_SERVICE_URL",
            value="http://localhost:8002",
            info="Base URL of the Marker container. Override with MARKER_SERVICE_URL.",
        ),
        IntInput(
            name="marker_service_timeout",
            display_name="MARKER_SERVICE_TIMEOUT",
            value=1800,
            advanced=True,
            info="Per-PDF timeout in seconds; large documents take minutes on the GPU.",
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="Documents", name="documents", method="load")]

    @staticmethod
    def _text_of(row):
        for key in ("text", "content", "page_content"):
            if row.get(key):
                return str(row[key])
        return ""

    @staticmethod
    def _source_of(row):
        for key in ("file_path", "source", "path", "file_name"):
            if row.get(key):
                return str(row[key])
        return "?"

    def _convert(self, path):
        """POST the PDF to the Marker container and return its Markdown."""
        import requests

        with open(path, "rb") as handle:
            response = requests.post(
                self.marker_service_url.rstrip("/") + "/convert",
                files={"file": (str(path).rsplit("/", 1)[-1], handle, "application/pdf")},
                timeout=self.marker_service_timeout,
            )
        response.raise_for_status()
        payload = response.json()
        if isinstance(payload, dict):
            return payload.get("markdown") or payload.get("text") or ""
        return str(payload)

    def load(self) -> DataFrame:
        rows = _rows(self.files)
        documents = []
        converted = 0
        failed = []
        for row in rows:
            source = self._source_of(row)
            text = self._text_of(row)
            if self.enable_marker_loader and source.lower().endswith(".pdf"):
                try:
                    markdown = self._convert(source)
                    if markdown:
                        text = markdown
                        converted += 1
                except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a failed conversion falls back
                    failed.append("{0}: {1}".format(source, type(exc).__name__))
            if text.strip():
                documents.append(Data(data={"text": text, "source": source}))

        self.status = "{0} file(s) | marker={1} | converted={2}{3}".format(
            len(rows),
            "on" if self.enable_marker_loader else "off",
            converted,
            "\nfailed: " + "; ".join(failed) if failed else "",
        )
        return DataFrame(data=documents)
'''


SPLITTER = HEADER + r'''

class RagIngestionSplitter(Component):
    display_name = "split_documents"
    description = (
        "ENABLE_CUSTOM_SPLITTER. Off: the recursive splitter packs paragraphs to CHUNK_SIZE "
        "with CHUNK_OVERLAP and drops anything under MIN_CHUNK_CHARS. On: the Marker-Markdown "
        "aware splitter packs whole structural units (headings, paragraphs, tables, fenced "
        "code) to CUSTOM_SPLITTER_CHUNK_SIZE and keeps smaller stubs down to "
        "CUSTOM_SPLITTER_MIN_CHUNK_CHARS. Every chunk is stamped with its source and a "
        "per-source chunk_seq - NAC's neighbour runs depend on that sequence."
    )
    icon = "scissors"
    name = "RagIngestionSplitter"

    inputs = [
        HandleInput(
            name="documents",
            display_name="Documents",
            input_types=["DataFrame", "Table", "Data"],
            is_list=True,
            required=True,
        ),
        BoolInput(
            name="enable_custom_splitter",
            display_name="ENABLE_CUSTOM_SPLITTER",
            value=False,
            info="False uses the recursive splitter, True the Markdown-structure splitter.",
        ),
        IntInput(name="chunk_size", display_name="CHUNK_SIZE", value=1000),
        IntInput(name="chunk_overlap", display_name="CHUNK_OVERLAP", value=200),
        IntInput(name="min_chunk_chars", display_name="MIN_CHUNK_CHARS", value=150),
        IntInput(
            name="custom_chunk_size",
            display_name="CUSTOM_SPLITTER_CHUNK_SIZE",
            value=1600,
            advanced=True,
        ),
        IntInput(
            name="custom_min_chunk_chars",
            display_name="CUSTOM_SPLITTER_MIN_CHUNK_CHARS",
            value=50,
            advanced=True,
        ),
    ]

    outputs = [Output(display_name="Chunks", name="chunks", method="split")]

    # A structural unit is a fenced code block, a table block, or a blank-line paragraph.
    _FENCE_RE = re.compile(r"```.*?```", re.DOTALL)

    @staticmethod
    def _text_of(row):
        for key in ("text", "content", "page_content"):
            if row.get(key):
                return str(row[key])
        return ""

    @staticmethod
    def _source_of(row):
        for key in ("source", "file_path", "path", "file_name"):
            if row.get(key):
                return str(row[key])
        return "?"

    def _units(self, text):
        """Split into structural units, keeping fenced code blocks whole."""
        units = []
        cursor = 0
        for match in self._FENCE_RE.finditer(text):
            before = text[cursor:match.start()]
            units.extend(part for part in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", before) if part.strip())
            units.append(match.group(0))
            cursor = match.end()
        units.extend(part for part in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", text[cursor:]) if part.strip())
        return [unit.strip() for unit in units if unit.strip()]

    def _split_custom(self, text):
        """Pack whole structural units up to CUSTOM_SPLITTER_CHUNK_SIZE.

        A heading starts a new chunk so a section never begins mid-chunk.
        """
        chunks = []
        current = []
        length = 0
        for unit in self._units(text):
            is_heading = unit.lstrip().startswith("#")
            if current and (is_heading or length + len(unit) + 2 > self.custom_chunk_size):
                chunks.append("\n\n".join(current))
                current = []
                length = 0
            current.append(unit)
            length += len(unit) + 2
        if current:
            chunks.append("\n\n".join(current))
        return [chunk for chunk in chunks if len(chunk) >= self.custom_min_chunk_chars]

    def _split_recursive(self, text):
        """Paragraph-then-sentence packing to CHUNK_SIZE with CHUNK_OVERLAP carry-over."""
        pieces = []
        for paragraph in re.split(r"\n\s*\n", text):
            paragraph = paragraph.strip()
            if not paragraph:
                continue
            if len(paragraph) <= self.chunk_size:
                pieces.append(paragraph)
                continue
            sentence = ""
            for part in re.split(r"(?<=[.!?])\s+", paragraph):
                if sentence and len(sentence) + len(part) + 1 > self.chunk_size:
                    pieces.append(sentence.strip())
                    sentence = ""
                sentence = (sentence + " " + part).strip()
            if sentence:
                pieces.append(sentence)

        chunks = []
        current = ""
        for piece in pieces:
            if current and len(current) + len(piece) + 2 > self.chunk_size:
                chunks.append(current)
                tail = current[-self.chunk_overlap:] if self.chunk_overlap > 0 else ""
                current = (tail + "\n\n" + piece).strip() if tail else piece
            else:
                current = (current + "\n\n" + piece).strip() if current else piece
        if current:
            chunks.append(current)
        return [chunk for chunk in chunks if len(chunk) >= self.min_chunk_chars]

    def split(self) -> DataFrame:
        rows = _rows(self.documents)
        output = []
        per_source = {}
        for row in rows:
            text = self._text_of(row)
            source = self._source_of(row)
            if not text.strip():
                continue
            pieces = (
                self._split_custom(text) if self.enable_custom_splitter else self._split_recursive(text)
            )
            for piece in pieces:
                sequence = per_source.get(source, 0)
                per_source[source] = sequence + 1
                output.append(
                    Data(data={"text": piece, "source": source, "chunk_seq": sequence})
                )

        sizes = [len(item.data["text"]) for item in output]
        self.status = "{0} document(s) -> {1} chunk(s) | splitter={2} | avg {3} chars".format(
            len(rows),
            len(output),
            "custom" if self.enable_custom_splitter else "recursive",
            int(sum(sizes) / len(sizes)) if sizes else 0,
        )
        return DataFrame(data=output)
'''

import json
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('flows/LangGraph RAG Pipeline.json'); f=json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
n=f['data']['nodes']; e=f['data']['edges']; ids={x['id'] for x in n}
assert len(ids)==len(n) and all(x['source'] in ids and x['target'] in ids for x in e)
print('KB:', round(p.stat().st_size/1024), '| components:', len([x for x in n if x['type']=='genericNode']), '| notes:', len([x for x in n if x['type']=='noteNode']), '| edges:', len(e))
print()
for x in n:
    if x['data'].get('type') in ('Directory','Knowledge','MemoryBase','RagMarkerLoader','RagIngestionSplitter'):

Two things: the error, then the ingestion branch.

The Playground error
Not a bug in the flow — those three dropdowns point at per-user resources stored in Langflow itself, not in the flow file, so they always arrive empty from an export. I checked: ~/.langflow/knowledge_bases doesn't exist on this machine, so no knowledge base has been created yet. Your own Vector Store RAG.json has the same empty knowledge_base / memory_base values for the same reason.

To clear it:

Knowledge — open the Knowledge dropdown on any Knowledge node and use Create new Knowledge Base (name, embedding model, DB provider). Make two: one for documents, one for the distilled QA pairs. Then select them on all four Knowledge nodes.
Memory Base — create one and attach it to this workflow, then select it. The component resolves it against the flow ID, so it must be attached, not just present. If you don't want long-term memory, delete the Memory Base node and its 5 edges — user_input and the cmd_* nodes take that input as optional, and there's a passing test for exactly that case.
Ingestion added
Seven new nodes across the top of the canvas, matching run_ingestion(enable_marker_loader, enable_custom_splitter):


discover_files → marker_loader → split_documents → Knowledge · documents (Ingest) → Ingestion Summary
discover_files — Langflow's Directory node, recursive over SUPPORTED_EXT, replacing discover_files() + the Unstructured loader.
marker_loader — ENABLE_MARKER_LOADER. On, each PDF is POSTed to MARKER_SERVICE_URL; off, Langflow's own extraction is used. A failed conversion falls back rather than dropping the file.
split_documents — ENABLE_CUSTOM_SPLITTER. Off: recursive at CHUNK_SIZE/CHUNK_OVERLAP/MIN_CHUNK_CHARS. On: Markdown-structure packing at CUSTOM_SPLITTER_CHUNK_SIZE, keeping stubs to CUSTOM_SPLITTER_MIN_CHUNK_CHARS, fenced code blocks intact.
The load-bearing detail: every chunk is stamped source + chunk_seq, and the Knowledge nodes' column config carries both into metadata. NAC's neighbour-run detection keys on a top-level int chunk_seq — ingest without it and NAC silently has nothing to merge.

I also closed the self-learning loop I'd flagged earlier: auto_distillation now has a second output shaped for ingestion, feeding Knowledge · learned_qa (Ingest), so distilled pairs are written back into the learned-QA base that the query pipeline retrieves from.

Verification: 106 checks pass. Writing the splitter tests caught a real bug — an oversized paragraph was never sub-split (3.8 KB chunks against a 1.6 KB budget) and headings were orphaned into sub-50-char fragments that the minimum-length filter then deleted, losing them entirely. Both fixed: oversized units are packed by sentence, and a heading-only buffer is never flushed alone. Graph.from_payload loads the flow: 52 vertices, 104 edges.

One caveat on the Marker loader: its HTTP contract (POST /convert, multipart file, JSON markdown) is reconstructed from the MARKER_SERVICE_URL comments in config.py — marker_service/ wasn't in what you shared. Check it before turning that switch on; the switch defaults to off. Same for ingestion_requests.py itself, which I haven't seen — if the real splitter differs from what I inferred from the config constants, send it and I'll align.

Run the ingestion branch on its own first, then query — the retrieval half only reads.
